   1: import 'dart:async';
   2: import 'dart:convert';
   3: import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
   4: import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
   5: import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
   6: import 'package:media_kit/media_kit.dart';
   7: import 'package:media_kit_video/media_kit_video.dart';
   8: import '../main.dart';
   9: import '../widgets/cinema_widgets.dart';
  10: import '../models/midia_model.dart';
  11: import '../services/supabase_service.dart';
  12: import '../services/torrserver_service.dart';
  13: import '../services/cdn_stream_service.dart';
  14: import '../services/catalog_audit_service.dart';
  15: 
  16: class CinemaPlayerScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  17:   final ObraAgrupada obra;
  18:   final FonteMidia fonteInicial;
  19:   final List<FonteMidia> listaEpisodios;
  20: 
  21:   const CinemaPlayerScreen({
  22:     super.key,
  23:     required this.obra,
  24:     required this.fonteInicial,
  25:     this.listaEpisodios = const [],
  26:   });
  27: 
  28:   @override
  29:   State<CinemaPlayerScreen> createState() => _CinemaPlayerScreenState();
  30: }
  31: 
  32: class _CinemaPlayerScreenState extends State<CinemaPlayerScreen> {
  33:   late final Player _player;
  34:   late final VideoController _videoCtrl;
  35:   late FonteMidia _fonteAtual;
  36: 
  37:   bool _controlesVisiveis = true;
  38:   bool _tentouBackup = false;
  39:   bool _carregandoStream = true;
  40:   String? _erroPlayer;
  41:   Timer? _hideTimer;
  42:   Timer? _saveProgressTimer;
  43: 
  44:   Duration _posicao = Duration.zero;
  45:   Duration _duracao = Duration.zero;
  46:   bool _tocando = true;
  47: 
  48:   // Configurações de Legenda
  49:   bool _legendaAtiva = true;
  50:   double _tamanhoLegenda = 28.0;
  51:   Color _corLegenda = Colors.white;
  52:   String _legendaSelecionada = 'Nenhuma';
  53: 
  54:   int _retryAutoCount = 0;
  55:   final Set<String> _fontesTentadas = {};
  56:   String? _osdTexto;
  57:   IconData? _osdIcone;
  58:   Timer? _osdTimer;
  59:   final FocusNode _playerRootFocus = FocusNode();
  60: 
  61:   @override
  62:   void initState() {
  63:     super.initState();
  64:     CinemaOrientation.emPlayer = true;
  65:     _fonteAtual = widget.fonteInicial;
  66:     SystemChrome.setEnabledSystemUIMode(SystemUiMode.immersiveSticky);
  67:     SystemChrome.setPreferredOrientations([
  68:       DeviceOrientation.landscapeLeft,
  69:       DeviceOrientation.landscapeRight,
  70:     ]);
  71: 
  72:     _player = Player(
  73:       configuration: const PlayerConfiguration(
  74:         bufferSize: 20 * 1024 * 1024,
  75:       ),
  76:     );
  77:     if (_player.platform is NativePlayer) {
  78:       final np = _player.platform as NativePlayer;
  79:       np.setProperty('demuxer-lavf-o', 'reconnect=1,reconnect_streamed=1,reconnect_delay_max=5');
  80:       np.setProperty('network-timeout', '30');
  81:     }
  82:     _videoCtrl = VideoController(_player);
  83: 
  84:     _player.stream.position.listen((p) {
  85:       if (mounted) setState(() => _posicao = p);
  86:     });
  87:     _player.stream.duration.listen((d) {
  88:       if (mounted) setState(() => _duracao = d);
  89:     });
  90:     _player.stream.playing.listen((pl) {
  91:       if (mounted) setState(() { _tocando = pl; if (pl) { _erroPlayer = null; _carregandoStream = false; } });
  92:     });
  93:     _player.stream.buffering.listen((b) {
  94:       if (mounted && !b) setState(() => _carregandoStream = false);
  95:     });
  96:     _player.stream.error.listen((err) {
  97:       if (!mounted) return;
  98:       final errLow = err.toLowerCase();
  99:       if (errLow.contains('could not open codec') || errLow.contains('subtitle') || (_tocando && _posicao.inSeconds > 1)) return;
 100:       if (_retryAutoCount < 1 && (_fonteAtual.urlStream.toLowerCase().startsWith('magnet:') || _fonteAtual.urlStream.toLowerCase().endsWith('.torrent'))) {
 101:         _retryAutoCount++;
 102:         Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 1400), () {
 103:           if (mounted) _abrirMidia(_fonteAtual.urlStream);
 104:         });
 105:         return;
 106:       }
 107:       if (!_tentouBackup && _fonteAtual.urlStreamBackup.isNotEmpty) {
 108:         _tentouBackup = true;
 109:         _abrirMidia(_fonteAtual.urlStreamBackup);
 110:       } else {
 111:         setState(() {
 112:           _carregandoStream = false;
 113:           _erroPlayer = 'Falha ao reproduzir stream: $err';
 114:         });
 115:       }
 116:     });
 117: 
 118:     _iniciarFonte(_fonteAtual);
 119:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 120: 
 121:     _saveProgressTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 12), (_) {
 122:       _salvarProgressoAtual();
 123:     });
 124:   }
 125: 
 126:   Future<void> _iniciarFonte(FonteMidia fonte, {bool limparHistoricoTentativas = true}) async {
 127:     if (limparHistoricoTentativas) _fontesTentadas.clear();
 128:     _fontesTentadas.add(fonte.id);
 129:     _retryAutoCount = 0;
 130:     try { await _player.stop(); } catch (_) {}
 131:     if (!mounted) return;
 132:     setState(() {
 133:       _fonteAtual = fonte;
 134:       _tentouBackup = false;
 135:       _carregandoStream = true;
 136:       _erroPlayer = null;
 137:       _osdTexto = null;
 138:     });
 139:     SupabaseService.registrarVisualizacao(fonte.id);
 140:     await _abrirMidia(fonte.urlStream);
 141:   }
 142: 
 143:   Future<bool> _tentarProximaFonteOuBackup(String motivoFalha) async {
 144:     if (!_tentouBackup && _fonteAtual.urlStreamBackup.isNotEmpty) {
 145:       _tentouBackup = true;
 146:       if (mounted) setState(() { _carregandoStream = true; _erroPlayer = null; });
 147:       await _abrirMidia(_fonteAtual.urlStreamBackup);
 148:       return true;
 149:     }
 150:     final alternativas = widget.obra.fontes.where((f) {
 151:       if (_fontesTentadas.contains(f.id)) return false;
 152:       if (_fonteAtual.episodio != null) return f.temporada == _fonteAtual.temporada && f.episodio == _fonteAtual.episodio;
 153:       return true;
 154:     }).toList();
 155:     if (alternativas.isNotEmpty) {
 156:       await _iniciarFonte(alternativas.first, limparHistoricoTentativas: false);
 157:       return true;
 158:     }
 159:     CatalogAuditService.instance.reportarProblemaParaScrape(
 160:       titulo: widget.obra.titulo,
 161:       categoria: 'link-sem-peers',
 162:       detalhes: 'Falha em todas as fontes de ${widget.obra.titulo} (${_fonteAtual.etiquetaEpisodio}): $motivoFalha',
 163:     );
 164:     return false;
 165:   }
 166: 
 167:   Future<void> _abrirMidia(String url) async {
 168:     try {
 169:       String urlFinal = url.trim();
 170:       final lower = urlFinal.toLowerCase();
 171:       if (lower.startsWith('magnet:?') || lower.endsWith('.torrent')) {
 172:         CdnResolucaoInfo? cdnInfo;
 173:         if (lower.startsWith('magnet:?')) {
 174:           cdnInfo = await CdnStreamService.resolverMagnet(urlFinal);
 175:         }
 176: 
 177:         if (!mounted) return;
 178: 
 179:         if (cdnInfo != null && cdnInfo.url.isNotEmpty) {
 180:           urlFinal = cdnInfo.url;
 181:         } else {
 182:           final swP2p = Stopwatch()..start();
 183:           final info = await TorrServerService.prepararMagnet(
 184:             urlFinal,
 185:             cancelado: () => !mounted,
 186:           );
 187:           swP2p.stop();
 188:           if (!mounted) return;
 189:           if (info == null) {
 190:             final motivo = TorrServerService.ultimoErro.isNotEmpty ? TorrServerService.ultimoErro : 'Não foi possível carregar o stream no momento.';
 191:             final trocou = await _tentarProximaFonteOuBackup(motivo);
 192:             if (!trocou && mounted) {
 193:               setState(() {
 194:                 _carregandoStream = false;
 195:                 _erroPlayer = '$motivo\nReportamos automaticamente para atualização no catálogo.';
 196:               });
 197:             }
 198:             return;
 199:           }
 200:           CdnStreamService.ultimoDiagnostico =
 201:               'Origem: P2P Local (TorrServer) | ${swP2p.elapsedMilliseconds}ms';
 202:           urlFinal = info.playUrl;
 203:         }
 204:       }
 205: 
 206:       final headers = Map<String, String>.from(_fonteAtual.headers);
 207:       if (!headers.containsKey('User-Agent')) {
 208:         headers['User-Agent'] = 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36';
 209:       }
 210:       try {
 211:         final platform = _player.platform;
 212:         if (platform is NativePlayer) {
 213:           await platform.setProperty('network-timeout', '30');
 214:           await platform.setProperty('demuxer-lavf-o', 'reconnect=1,reconnect_streamed=1,reconnect_delay_max=5');
 215:           await platform.setProperty('cache', 'yes');
 216:           await platform.setProperty('demuxer-max-bytes', '33554432');
 217:         }
 218:       } catch (_) {}
 219:       await _player.open(Media(urlFinal, httpHeaders: headers), play: true);
 220:       await _player.play();
 221:     } catch (e) {
 222:       if (mounted) {
 223:         setState(() {
 224:           _carregandoStream = false;
 225:           _erroPlayer = e.toString();
 226:         });
 227:       }
 228:     }
 229:   }
 230: 
 231:   Future<void> _salvarProgressoAtual() async {
 232:     if (_duracao.inSeconds <= 10) return;
 233:     final pct = _posicao.inSeconds / _duracao.inSeconds;
 234:     await SupabaseService.salvarProgressoAssistido(
 235:       chaveGrupo: widget.obra.chaveGrupo,
 236:       progresso: pct,
 237:       etiquetaEp: _fonteAtual.etiquetaEpisodio,
 238:     );
 239:   }
 240: 
 241:   void _reiniciarTimerControles() {
 242:     _hideTimer?.cancel();
 243:     if (!mounted) return;
 244:     setState(() => _controlesVisiveis = true);
 245:     _hideTimer = Timer(const Duration(seconds: 5), () {
 246:       if (mounted && _tocando) {
 247:         setState(() => _controlesVisiveis = false);
 248:       }
 249:     });
 250:   }
 251: 
 252:   FonteMidia? get _proximoEpisodio {
 253:     if (widget.listaEpisodios.isEmpty) return null;
 254:     final idx = widget.listaEpisodios.indexWhere((e) => e.id == _fonteAtual.id);
 255:     if (idx >= 0 && idx + 1 < widget.listaEpisodios.length) {
 256:       return widget.listaEpisodios[idx + 1];
 257:     }
 258:     return null;
 259:   }
 260: 
 261:   void _pularAbertura() {
 262:     final salto = Duration(seconds: widget.obra.aberturaSegundos);
 263:     _player.seek(_posicao + salto);
 264:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 265:   }
 266: 
 267:   String _formatarTempo(Duration d) {
 268:     final h = d.inHours;
 269:     final m = d.inMinutes.remainder(60).toString().padLeft(2, '0');
 270:     final s = d.inSeconds.remainder(60).toString().padLeft(2, '0');
 271:     return h > 0 ? '$h:$m:$s' : '$m:$s';
 272:   }
 273: 
 274:   @override
 275:   void dispose() {
 276:     CinemaOrientation.emPlayer = false;
 277:     _salvarProgressoAtual();
 278:     _hideTimer?.cancel();
 279:     _osdTimer?.cancel();
 280:     _saveProgressTimer?.cancel();
 281:     _playerRootFocus.dispose();
 282:     _player.dispose();
 283:     SystemChrome.setEnabledSystemUIMode(SystemUiMode.edgeToEdge);
 284:     SystemChrome.setPreferredOrientations(DeviceOrientation.values);
 285:     super.dispose();
 286:   }
 287: 
 288:   void _abrirModalLegendas() {
 289:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 290:     final buscaCtrl = TextEditingController(text: widget.obra.titulo);
 291:     List<Map<String, dynamic>> resultadosSub = [];
 292:     bool carregandoSub = true;
 293:     bool jaBuscou = false;
 294:     bool iniciouAuto = false;
 295: 
 296:     showModalBottomSheet(
 297:       context: context,
 298:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF141822),
 299:       isScrollControlled: true,
 300:       builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
 301:         builder: (ctx, setModal) {
 302:           Future<String?> descobrirImdbId(String titulo) async {
 303:             try {
 304:               final limpo = titulo.replaceAll(RegExp(r'\(\d{4}\)'), '').trim();
 305:               if (limpo.isEmpty) return null;
 306:               final firstChar = limpo[0].toLowerCase().replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z0-9]'), 'x');
 307:               final u = Uri.parse('https://v3.sg.media-imdb.com/suggestion/$firstChar/${Uri.encodeComponent(limpo)}.json');
 308:               final r = await http.get(u, headers: {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 309:               if (r.statusCode == 200) {
 310:                 final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(r.bodyBytes));
 311:                 final d = (data is Map ? data['d'] : null) as List<dynamic>? ?? [];
 312:                 for (final item in d) {
 313:                   if (item is Map) {
 314:                     final id = (item['id'] ?? '').toString();
 315:                     if (id.startsWith('tt')) return id;
 316:                   }
 317:                 }
 318:               }
 319:             } catch (_) {}
 320:             return null;
 321:           }
 322: 
 323:           Future<void> buscarMultiBancos() async {
 324:             final q = buscaCtrl.text.trim();
 325:             if (q.isEmpty) return;
 326:             setModal(() {
 327:               carregandoSub = true;
 328:               jaBuscou = true;
 329:               resultadosSub = [];
 330:             });
 331:             final encontrados = <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
 332: 
 333:             final imdbId = await descobrirImdbId(q);
 334:             final temp = _fonteAtual.temporada ?? 1;
 335:             final ep = _fonteAtual.episodio ?? 1;
 336: 
 337:             // 1. OpenSubtitles V3 via IMDb ID (retorna .srt/.vtt diretos em PT/EN/ES)
 338:             if (imdbId != null && imdbId.startsWith('tt')) {
 339:               try {
 340:                 final tipo = widget.obra.isSerie ? 'series' : 'movie';
 341:                 final idQuery = widget.obra.isSerie ? '$imdbId:$temp:$ep' : imdbId;
 342:                 final uOs3 = Uri.parse('https://opensubtitles-v3.strem.io/subtitles/$tipo/$idQuery.json');
 343:                 final rOs3 = await http.get(uOs3).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 6));
 344:                 if (rOs3.statusCode == 200) {
 345:                   final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(rOs3.bodyBytes));
 346:                   final subs = (data is Map ? data['subtitles'] : null) as List<dynamic>? ?? [];
 347:                   for (final s in subs) {
 348:                     if (s is Map && (s['url'] ?? '').toString().isNotEmpty) {
 349:                       final lang = (s['lang'] ?? 'por').toString();
 350:                       final langLow = lang.toLowerCase();
 351:                       if (langLow.contains('por') || langLow.contains('pob') || langLow == 'pt' || langLow.contains('eng') || langLow.contains('spa')) {
 352:                         encontrados.add({
 353:                           'SubFileName': '${widget.obra.titulo} (${lang.toUpperCase()}) [OpenSubtitles]',
 354:                           'LanguageName': lang.toUpperCase(),
 355:                           'SubDownloadLink': s['url'].toString(),
 356:                           'Prioridade': (langLow.contains('por') || langLow.contains('pob') || langLow == 'pt') ? 0 : 1,
 357:                         });
 358:                       }
 359:                     }
 360:                   }
 361:                 }
 362:               } catch (_) {}
 363: 
 364:               // 2. Wyzie / SubSource API via IMDb ID (legendas SRT limpas)
 365:               try {
 366:                 final uWy = widget.obra.isSerie
 367:                     ? Uri.parse('https://sub.wyzie.ru/search?id=$imdbId&season=$temp&episode=$ep')
 368:                     : Uri.parse('https://sub.wyzie.ru/search?id=$imdbId');
 369:                 final rWy = await http.get(uWy).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 6));
 370:                 if (rWy.statusCode == 200) {
 371:                   final list = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(rWy.bodyBytes));
 372:                   if (list is List) {
 373:                     for (final s in list) {
 374:                       if (s is Map && (s['url'] ?? '').toString().isNotEmpty) {
 375:                         final lang = (s['display'] ?? s['language'] ?? 'PT').toString();
 376:                         final langLow = lang.toLowerCase();
 377:                         encontrados.add({
 378:                           'SubFileName': '${s['release'] ?? widget.obra.titulo} ($lang) [SubSource]',
 379:                           'LanguageName': lang,
 380:                           'SubDownloadLink': s['url'].toString(),
 381:                           'Prioridade': (langLow.contains('portug') || langLow == 'pt' || langLow == 'pb') ? 0 : 1,
 382:                         });
 383:                       }
 384:                     }
 385:                   }
 386:                 }
 387:               } catch (_) {}
 388:             }
 389: 
 390:             // Ordena colocando Portugues no topo
 391:             encontrados.sort((a, b) => (a['Prioridade'] as int).compareTo(b['Prioridade'] as int));
 392: 
 393:             if (ctx.mounted) {
 394:               setModal(() {
 395:                 resultadosSub = encontrados.take(15).toList();
 396:                 carregandoSub = false;
 397:               });
 398:             }
 399:           }
 400: 
 401:           if (!iniciouAuto) {
 402:             iniciouAuto = true;
 403:             Future.microtask(buscarMultiBancos);
 404:           }
 405: 
 406:           return Padding(
 407:             padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 16, 20, MediaQuery.of(ctx).viewInsets.bottom + 20),
 408:             child: SizedBox(
 409:               height: 380,
 410:               child: Column(
 411:                 crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
 412:                 children: [
 413:                   Row(
 414:                     children: [
 415:                       const Expanded(
 416:                         child: Text(
 417:                           'Legendas Online (OpenSubtitles & SubSource)',
 418:                           style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 15.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
 419:                         ),
 420:                       ),
 421:                       Switch(
 422:                         value: _legendaAtiva,
 423:                         activeColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E),
 424:                         onChanged: (v) {
 425:                           setState(() => _legendaAtiva = v);
 426:                           setModal(() {});
 427:                           if (!v) _player.setSubtitleTrack(SubtitleTrack.no());
 428:                         },
 429:                       ),
 430:                     ],
 431:                   ),
 432:                   Row(
 433:                     children: [
 434:                       const Text('Tamanho:', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)),
 435:                       Expanded(
 436:                         child: Slider(
 437:                           value: _tamanhoLegenda,
 438:                           min: 18,
 439:                           max: 44,
 440:                           activeColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E),
 441:                           onChanged: (v) {
 442:                             setState(() => _tamanhoLegenda = v);
 443:                             setModal(() {});
 444:                           },
 445:                         ),
 446:                       ),
 447:                       ...[Colors.white, const Color(0xFFF5B82E), Colors.cyanAccent].map(
 448:                         (c) => GestureDetector(
 449:                           onTap: () {
 450:                             setState(() => _corLegenda = c);
 451:                             setModal(() {});
 452:                           },
 453:                           child: Container(
 454:                             width: 24,
 455:                             height: 24,
 456:                             margin: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
 457:                             decoration: BoxDecoration(
 458:                               color: c,
 459:                               shape: BoxShape.circle,
 460:                               border: Border.all(color: _corLegenda == c ? Colors.redAccent : Colors.white54, width: 2),
 461:                             ),
 462:                           ),
 463:                         ),
 464:                       ),
 465:                     ],
 466:                   ),
 467:                   const SizedBox(height: 8),
 468:                   Row(
 469:                     children: [
 470:                       Expanded(
 471:                         child: TextField(
 472:                           controller: buscaCtrl,
 473:                           style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13.5),
 474:                           decoration: InputDecoration(
 475:                             hintText: 'Título do filme ou série...',
 476:                             filled: true,
 477:                             fillColor: Colors.white10,
 478:                             isDense: true,
 479:                             border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
 480:                           ),
 481:                         ),
 482:                       ),
 483:                       const SizedBox(width: 8),
 484:                       ElevatedButton.icon(
 485:                         style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E), foregroundColor: Colors.black),
 486:                         onPressed: buscarMultiBancos,
 487:                         icon: const Icon(Icons.search, size: 18),
 488:                         label: const Text('Buscar'),
 489:                       ),
 490:                     ],
 491:                   ),
 492:                   const SizedBox(height: 10),
 493:                   Expanded(
 494:                     child: carregandoSub
 495:                         ? const Center(
 496:                             child: Column(
 497:                               mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
 498:                               children: [
 499:                                 CircularProgressIndicator(color: Color(0xFFF5B82E)),
 500:                                 SizedBox(height: 10),
 501:                                 Text('Buscando legendas em Português...', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 13)),
 502:                               ],
 503:                             ),
 504:                           )
 505:                         : resultadosSub.isEmpty
 506:                             ? Center(
 507:                                 child: Column(
 508:                                   mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
 509:                                   children: [
 510:                                     if (jaBuscou)
 511:                                       Container(
 512:                                         margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
 513:                                         padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
 514:                                         decoration: BoxDecoration(
 515:                                           color: Colors.redAccent.withOpacity(0.16),
 516:                                           borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
 517:                                           border: Border.all(color: Colors.redAccent.withOpacity(0.5)),
 518:                                         ),
 519:                                         child: const Row(
 520:                                           mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
 521:                                           children: [
 522:                                             Icon(Icons.subtitles_off, color: Colors.redAccent, size: 18),
 523:                                             SizedBox(width: 8),
 524:                                             Text(
 525:                                               'Legenda não encontrada para este título.',
 526:                                               style: TextStyle(color: Colors.redAccent, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 13),
 527:                                             ),
 528:                                           ],
 529:                                         ),
 530:                                       ),
 531:                                     Text(
 532:                                       'Legenda ativa: $_legendaSelecionada\nTente buscar pelo título original em inglês se necessário.',
 533:                                       textAlign: TextAlign.center,
 534:                                       style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 12.5),
 535:                                     ),
 536:                                   ],
 537:                                 ),
 538:                               )
 539:                             : Column(
 540:                                 crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
 541:                                 children: [
 542:                                   Container(
 543:                                     margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
 544:                                     padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
 545:                                     decoration: BoxDecoration(
 546:                                       color: Colors.greenAccent.withOpacity(0.14),
 547:                                       borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
 548:                                       border: Border.all(color: Colors.greenAccent.withOpacity(0.5)),
 549:                                     ),
 550:                                     child: Row(
 551:                                       children: [
 552:                                         const Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.greenAccent, size: 17),
 553:                                         const SizedBox(width: 8),
 554:                                         Text(
 555:                                           'Legenda encontrada! (${resultadosSub.length} opções)',
 556:                                           style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.greenAccent, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 12.5),
 557:                                         ),
 558:                                       ],
 559:                                     ),
 560:                                   ),
 561:                                   Expanded(
 562:                                     child: ListView.builder(
 563:                                       itemCount: resultadosSub.length,
 564:                                       itemBuilder: (_, i) {
 565:                                         final item = resultadosSub[i];
 566:                                         final nome = (item['SubFileName'] ?? 'Legenda').toString();
 567:                                         final idioma = (item['LanguageName'] ?? '').toString();
 568:                                         final dl = (item['SubDownloadLink'] ?? '').toString();
 569:                                         return CinemaFocusBox(
 570:                                           borderRadius: 8,
 571:                                           onTap: () async {
 572:                                             if (dl.isNotEmpty) {
 573:                                               await _player.setSubtitleTrack(SubtitleTrack.uri(dl, title: nome, language: idioma));
 574:                                               setState(() { _legendaAtiva = true; _legendaSelecionada = nome; });
 575:                                             }
 576:                                             if (ctx.mounted) Navigator.pop(ctx);
 577:                                           },
 578:                                           child: ListTile(
 579:                                           dense: true,
 580:                                           leading: const Icon(Icons.subtitles, color: Color(0xFFF5B82E)),
 581:                                           title: Text(nome, maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13)),
 582:                                           subtitle: Text(idioma, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 11)),
 583:                                           onTap: () async {
 584:                                             if (dl.isNotEmpty) {
 585:                                               await _player.setSubtitleTrack(SubtitleTrack.uri(dl, title: nome, language: idioma));
 586:                                               setState(() {
 587:                                                 _legendaAtiva = true;
 588:                                                 _legendaSelecionada = nome;
 589:                                               });
 590:                                             }
 591:                                             if (ctx.mounted) Navigator.pop(ctx);
 592:                                           },
 593:                                         )
 594:                                         );
 595:                                       },
 596:                                     ),
 597:                                   ),
 598:                                 ],
 599:                               ),
 600:                   ),
 601:                 ],
 602:               ),
 603:             ),
 604:           );
 605:         },
 606:       ),
 607:     );
 608:   }

 610:   void _abrirModalFontes() {
 611:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 612:     final fontesMesmoEp = widget.obra.fontes.where((f) {
 613:       if (_fonteAtual.episodio != null) {
 614:         return f.temporada == _fonteAtual.temporada && f.episodio == _fonteAtual.episodio;
 615:       }
 616:       return true;
 617:     }).toList();
 618: 
 619:     showModalBottomSheet(
 620:       context: context,
 621:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF141822),
 622:       builder: (ctx) => ListView(
 623:         padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
 624:         children: [
 625:           const Text('Resolução e Áudio', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
 626:           const SizedBox(height: 12),
 627:           ...fontesMesmoEp.map(
 628:             (f) => ListTile(
 629:               leading: Icon(
 630:                 f.id == _fonteAtual.id ? Icons.check_circle : Icons.play_circle_outline,
 631:                 color: f.id == _fonteAtual.id ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.white54,
 632:               ),
 633:               title: Text(f.badgeResumo, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
 634:               subtitle: Text(f.formato.toUpperCase(), style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)),
 635:               onTap: () {
 636:                 Navigator.pop(ctx);
 637:                 _iniciarFonte(f);
 638:               },
 639:             ),
 640:           ),
 641:         ],
 642:       ),
 643:     );
 644:   }
 645: 
 646:   void _abrirModalEpisodios() {
 647:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 648:     showModalBottomSheet(
 649:       context: context,
 650:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF141822),
 651:       builder: (ctx) => ListView.builder(
 652:         padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
 653:         itemCount: widget.listaEpisodios.length,
 654:         itemBuilder: (_, idx) {
 655:           final ep = widget.listaEpisodios[idx];
 656:           final atual = ep.id == _fonteAtual.id;
 657:           return ListTile(
 658:             leading: Text(
 659:               ep.etiquetaEpisodio.isNotEmpty ? ep.etiquetaEpisodio : 'Ep ${idx + 1}',
 660:               style: TextStyle(color: atual ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.white70, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
 661:             ),
 662:             title: Text(ep.titulo, maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
 663:             trailing: Text(ep.resolucao, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 12)),
 664:             onTap: () {
 665:               Navigator.pop(ctx);
 666:               _iniciarFonte(ep);
 667:             },
 668:           );
 669:         },
 670:       ),
 671:     );
 672:   }
 673: 
 674:   void _mostrarOsd(IconData icone, String texto) {
 675:     _osdTimer?.cancel();
 676:     if (!mounted) return;
 677:     setState(() {
 678:       _osdIcone = icone;
 679:       _osdTexto = texto;
 680:     });
 681:     _osdTimer = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 800), () {
 682:       if (mounted) {
 683:         setState(() {
 684:           _osdTexto = null;
 685:           _osdIcone = null;
 686:         });
 687:       }
 688:     });
 689:   }
 690: 
 691:   void _alternarPlayPause() {
 692:     _player.playOrPause();
 693:     final vaiPausar = _tocando;
 694:     _mostrarOsd(vaiPausar ? Icons.pause_circle_filled : Icons.play_circle_filled, vaiPausar ? 'Pausado' : 'Reproduzindo');
 695:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 696:   }
 697: 
 698:   void _buscarRelativo(int segundos) {
 699:     final alvo = _posicao + Duration(seconds: segundos);
 700:     final clamped = alvo < Duration.zero
 701:         ? Duration.zero
 702:         : (_duracao > Duration.zero && alvo > _duracao ? _duracao : alvo);
 703:     _player.seek(clamped);
 704:     _mostrarOsd(
 705:       segundos < 0 ? Icons.replay_10 : Icons.forward_10,
 706:       segundos < 0 ? '${segundos}s' : '+${segundos}s',
 707:     );
 708:     _reiniciarTimerControles();
 709:   }
 710: 
 711:   bool _isConfirmKey(LogicalKeyboardKey k) {
 712:     return k == LogicalKeyboardKey.select ||
 713:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.enter ||
 714:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.numpadEnter ||
 715:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.accept ||
 716:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.gameButtonA ||
 717:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.space ||
 718:         k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaPlayPause;
 719:   }
 720: 
 721:   @override
 722:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 723:     final podePularAbertura = widget.obra.isSerie &&
 724:         _erroPlayer == null &&
 725:         widget.obra.aberturaSegundos > 0 &&
 726:         _posicao.inSeconds >= 5 &&
 727:         _posicao.inSeconds <= 22;
 728:     final prox = _proximoEpisodio;
 729: 
 730:     return Scaffold(
 731:       backgroundColor: Colors.black,
 732:       body: Focus(
 733:         focusNode: _playerRootFocus,
 734:         autofocus: true,
 735:         onKeyEvent: (node, event) {
 736:           if (event is! KeyDownEvent) return KeyEventResult.ignored;
 737:           final k = event.logicalKey;
 738: 
 739:           if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaPlayPause ||
 740:               k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaPlay ||
 741:               k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaPause) {
 742:             _alternarPlayPause();
 743:             return KeyEventResult.handled;
 744:           }
 745:           if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaFastForward) {
 746:             _buscarRelativo(10);
 747:             return KeyEventResult.handled;
 748:           }
 749:           if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaRewind) {
 750:             _buscarRelativo(-10);
 751:             return KeyEventResult.handled;
 752:           }
 753: 
 754:           if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.escape || k == LogicalKeyboardKey.goBack) {
 755:             if (_controlesVisiveis && !_carregandoStream) {
 756:               setState(() => _controlesVisiveis = false);
 757:               _playerRootFocus.requestFocus();
 758:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 759:             }
 760:             Navigator.pop(context);
 761:             return KeyEventResult.handled;
 762:           }
 763: 
 764:           if (!_controlesVisiveis) {
 765:             if (_isConfirmKey(k)) {
 766:               _alternarPlayPause();
 767:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 768:             }
 769:             if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowRight) {
 770:               _buscarRelativo(10);
 771:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 772:             }
 773:             if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowLeft) {
 774:               _buscarRelativo(-10);
 775:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 776:             }
 777:             if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowUp || k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowDown) {
 778:               _reiniciarTimerControles();
 779:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 780:             }
 781:           } else {
 782:             _reiniciarTimerControles();
 783:             if (_playerRootFocus.hasPrimaryFocus) {
 784:               if (_isConfirmKey(k)) {
 785:                 _alternarPlayPause();
 786:                 return KeyEventResult.handled;
 787:               }
 788:               if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowRight) {
 789:                 _buscarRelativo(10);
 790:                 return KeyEventResult.handled;
 791:               }
 792:               if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowLeft) {
 793:                 _buscarRelativo(-10);
 794:                 return KeyEventResult.handled;
 795:               }
 796:               if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowDown || k == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowUp) {
 797:                 FocusScope.of(context).nextFocus();
 798:                 return KeyEventResult.handled;
 799:               }
 800:             }
 801:           }
 802:           return KeyEventResult.ignored;
 803:         },
 804:         child: GestureDetector(
 805:           onTap: () {
 806:             if (_controlesVisiveis && !_carregandoStream) {
 807:               setState(() => _controlesVisiveis = false);
 808:               _playerRootFocus.requestFocus();
 809:             } else {
 810:               _reiniciarTimerControles();
 811:             }
 812:           },
 813:           child: Stack(
 814:             fit: StackFit.expand,

  43:   @override
  44:   void initState() {
  45:     super.initState();
  46:     _atualizarPopulares();
  47:   }

  49:   void _atualizarPopulares() {
  50:     final ordenados = List<ObraAgrupada>.from(widget.catalogo)
  51:       ..sort((a, b) => b.scorePopularidade.compareTo(a.scorePopularidade));
  52:     final top = ordenados.take(30).toList()..shuffle();
  53:     setState(() => _populares = top.take(10).toList());
  54:   }
  55: 
  56:   Future<void> _executarBusca() async {
  57:     final q = _ctrl.text.trim();
  58:     if (q.isEmpty) {
  59:       setState(() => _resultados = []);
  60:       return;
  61:     }
  62:     setState(() => _buscando = true);
  63:     final res = await SupabaseService.buscarComElencoEDiretores(q, widget.catalogo);
  64:     if (mounted) {
  65:       setState(() {
  66:         _resultados = res;
  67:         _buscando = false;
  68:       });
  69:     }
  70:   }
  71: 
  72:   void _digitar(String char) {
  73:     _ctrl.text = '${_ctrl.text}$char';
  74:     _executarBusca();
  75:   }
  76: 
  77:   void _apagarUltimo() {
  78:     if (_ctrl.text.isNotEmpty) {
  79:       _ctrl.text = _ctrl.text.substring(0, _ctrl.text.length - 1);
  80:       _executarBusca();
  81:     }
  82:   }
  83: 
  84:   void _limpar() {
  85:     _ctrl.clear();
  86:     setState(() => _resultados = []);
  87:   }
  88: 
  89:   @override
  90:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {

 117:                   autofocus: true,
 118:                   textInputAction: TextInputAction.search,
 119:                   onEditingComplete: () => FocusScope.of(context).nextFocus(),
 120:                   onChanged: (_) {
 121:                   setState(() {});
 122:                   _executarBusca();
 123:                 },
 124:                 style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 15),
 125:                 decoration: InputDecoration(
 126:                   hintText: 'Pesquise filmes, séries ou diretores...',
 127:                   hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 14),
 128:                   prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, color: Color(0xFFF5B82E)),
 129:                   suffixIcon: emBusca
 130:                       ? IconButton(
 131:                           icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.white70),
 132:                           onPressed: _limpar,
 133:                         )
 134:                       : null,
 135:                   filled: true,
 136:                   fillColor: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.12),
 137:                   border: OutlineInputBorder(
 138:                     borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
 139:                     borderSide: BorderSide.none,
 140:                   ),
 141:                   contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 12),
 142:                 ),
 143:               ),
 144:                 ),
 145:               const SizedBox(height: 12),
 146:               Row(
 147:                 children: [
 148:                   Text(
 149:                     emBusca ? 'Resultados da busca' : 'Buscas populares',
 150:                     style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
 151:                   ),
 152:                   const SizedBox(width: 8),
 153:                   if (!emBusca)
 154:                     InkWell(
 155:                       onTap: _atualizarPopulares,
 156:                       child: const Icon(Icons.refresh, color: Color(0xFFF5B82E), size: 18),
 157:                     ),
 158:                   const Spacer(),
 159:                   Text(
 160:                     'Total: ${listaExibida.length}',
 161:                     style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
 162:                   ),
 163:                 ],
 164:               ),
 165:               const SizedBox(height: 10),
 166:               if (_buscando)
 167:                 const Expanded(child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Color(0xFFF5B82E))))
 168:               else if (listaExibida.isEmpty)
 169:                 const Expanded(
 170:                   child: Center(
 171:                     child: Text('Nenhum filme, série ou diretor encontrado.', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 15)),
 172:                   ),
 173:                 )
 174:               else
 175:                 Expanded(
 176:                   child: GridView.builder(
 177:                     keyboardDismissBehavior: ScrollViewKeyboardDismissBehavior.onDrag,
 178:                     gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
 179:                       crossAxisCount: 3,
 180:                       childAspectRatio: 0.60,
 181:                       crossAxisSpacing: 10,
 182:                       mainAxisSpacing: 10,
 183:                     ),
 184:                     itemCount: listaExibida.length,
 185:                     itemBuilder: (_, idx) => TvPosterCard(
 186:                       obra: listaExibida[idx],
 187:                       onTap: () => widget.onAbrirObra(listaExibida[idx]),
 188:                     ),
 189:                   ),
 190:                 ),
 191:             ],
 192:           ),
 193:         ),
 194:       );
 195:     }
 196: 
 197:     return Scaffold(
 198:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0E141B),
 199:       appBar: AppBar(
 200:         backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
 201:         title: const Text('Pesquisar', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
 202:       ),
 203:       body: Row(
 204:         children: [
 205:           Container(
 206:             width: 340,
 207:             padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 10, 20, 16),
 208:             color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.28),
 209:             child: Column(
 210:               children: [
 211:                 TextField(
 212:                   controller: _ctrl,
 213:                   onChanged: (_) => _executarBusca(),
 214:                   style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 15),
 215:                   decoration: InputDecoration(
 216:                     hintText: 'Pesquise filmes, séries ou diretores.',
 217:                     hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 13.5),

 243:                       ),

 369:   Widget _teclaEspecial({String? label, IconData? icon, required VoidCallback onTap}) {
 370:     return CinemaFocusBox(
 371:       onTap: onTap,
 372:       borderRadius: 6,
 373:       scaleOnFocus: 1.07,
 374:       child: Container(
 375:         padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 9),

 377:         child: label != null
 378:             ? Text(label, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))
 379:             : Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 19),

 381:     );

 384: 
 385: class TvFiltroScreen extends StatefulWidget {
 386:   final List<ObraAgrupada> catalogo;
 387:   final String tipoInicial;
 388:   final void Function(ObraAgrupada) onAbrirObra;
 389: 
 390:   const TvFiltroScreen({
 391:     super.key,
 392:     required this.catalogo,
 393:     this.tipoInicial = 'Filmes',
 394:     required this.onAbrirObra,
 395:   });
 396: 
 397:   @override
 398:   State<TvFiltroScreen> createState() => _TvFiltroScreenState();
 399: }
 400: 
 401: class _TvFiltroScreenState extends State<TvFiltroScreen> {
 402:   late String _tipo;
 403:   String _genero = 'Todos';
 404:   String _ano = 'Todos';
 405:   String _pais = 'Todos';
 406:   String _audio = 'Todos';
 407: 
 408:   static const List<String> _tipos = ['Filmes', 'Séries', 'Kids', 'Anime'];
 409:   static const List<String> _generos = [
 410:     'Todos', 'Ação', 'Drama', 'Aventura', 'Crime',
 411:     'Ficção científica', 'Desenho', 'Comédia', 'Romance',
 412:     'Animação', 'Suspense', 'Terror', 'Mistério',
 413:   ];
 414:   static const List<String> _anos = [
 415:     'Todos', '2026', '2025', '2024', '2023', '2022',
 416:     '2021', '2020', '2019', '2018', '2017', 'Anteriores',
 417:   ];
 418:   static const List<String> _paises = [
 419:     'Todos', 'Brasil', 'Portugal', 'Estados Unidos', 'México',
 420:     'Reino Unido', 'Japão', 'Coreia do Sul', 'Espanha', 'Alemanha',
 421:   ];
 422:   static const List<String> _audios = ['Todos', 'Português', 'Espanhol', 'Inglês'];
 423: 
 424:   @override
 425:   void initState() {
 426:     super.initState();
 427:     _tipo = widget.tipoInicial;
 428:   }
 429: 
 430:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _filtrados {
 431:     return widget.catalogo.where((o) {
 432:       final cat = o.categoria.toLowerCase();
 433:       final gen = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 434:       final tags = o.tags.map((t) => SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(t)).toList();
 435: 
 436:       if (_tipo == 'Filmes' && (!cat.contains('filme') || cat.contains('série') || cat.contains('serie'))) return false;
 437:       if (_tipo == 'Séries' && !(cat.contains('série') || cat.contains('serie'))) return false;
 438:       if (_tipo == 'Kids' && !(cat.contains('infantil') || cat.contains('kids') || gen.contains('infantil') || gen.contains('animacao') || tags.contains('kids'))) return false;
 439:       if (_tipo == 'Anime' && !(cat.contains('anime') || gen.contains('anime') || tags.contains('anime'))) return false;
 440: 
 441:       if (_genero != 'Todos') {
 442:         final gBusca = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(_genero);
 443:         if (!gen.contains(gBusca) && !tags.any((t) => t.contains(gBusca))) return false;
 444:       }
 445: 
 446:       if (_ano != 'Todos') {
 447:         if (_ano == 'Anteriores') {
 448:           final y = int.tryParse(o.ano.trim()) ?? 9999;
 449:           if (y >= 2017) return false;
 450:         } else if (!o.ano.contains(_ano)) {
 451:           return false;
 452:         }
 453:       }
 454: 
 455:       if (_pais != 'Todos') {
 456:         final pNorm = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.pais);
 457:         final pBusca = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(_pais);
 458:         if (pNorm.isNotEmpty && !pNorm.contains(pBusca)) return false;
 459:       }
 460: 
 461:       if (_audio != 'Todos') {
 462:         final aBusca = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(_audio);
 463:         final temAudio = o.audiosDisponiveis.any((a) {
 464:           final an = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(a);
 465:           if (aBusca == 'portugues') return an.contains('portugues') || an.contains('dublado') || an.contains('nacional') || an == 'pt';
 466:           if (aBusca == 'ingles') return an.contains('ingles') || an.contains('legendado') || an == 'en';
 467:           return an.contains(aBusca);
 468:         });
 469:         if (!temAudio) return false;
 470:       }
 471:       return true;
 472:     }).toList();
 473:   }
 474: 
 475:   Widget _linhaFiltro({required String titulo, required List<String> opcoes, required String selecionado, required void Function(String) onSelect}) {
 476:     return SizedBox(
 477:       height: 36,
 478:       child: Row(
 479:         children: [
 480:           SizedBox(width: 72, child: Text('$titulo:', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))),
 481:           Expanded(
 482:             child: ListView.separated(
 483:               scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
 484:               itemCount: opcoes.length,
 485:               separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 8),
 486:               itemBuilder: (_, i) {
 487:                 final item = opcoes[i];
 488:                 final ativo = item == selecionado;
 489:                 return CinemaFocusBox(
 490:                   onTap: () => onSelect(item),
 491:                   borderRadius: 18,
 492:                   child: Container(
 493:                     padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 6),
 494:                     decoration: BoxDecoration(color: ativo ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.transparent, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18)),
 495:                     alignment: Alignment.center,
 496:                     child: Text(item, style: TextStyle(color: ativo ? Colors.black : Colors.white, fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: ativo ? FontWeight.w800 : FontWeight.w500)),
 497:                   ),
 498:                 );
 499:               },
 500:             ),
 501:           ),
 502:         ],
 503:       ),
 504:     );
 505:   }
 506: 
 507:   @override
 508:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 509:     final lista = _filtrados;
 510:     return Scaffold(
 511:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0E141B),
 512:       appBar: AppBar(
 513:         backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
 514:         title: const Text('Filtro Avançado', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
 515:         actions: [
 516:           Padding(
 517:             padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 20),
 518:             child: Center(child: Text('${lista.length} encontrados', style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFF5B82E), fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))),
 519:           ),
 520:         ],
 521:       ),
 522:       body: Padding(
 523:         padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 6, 24, 16),
 524:         child: Column(
 525:           children: [
 526:             _linhaFiltro(titulo: 'Tipo', opcoes: _tipos, selecionado: _tipo, onSelect: (v) => setState(() => _tipo = v)),
 527:             const SizedBox(height: 6),
 528:             _linhaFiltro(titulo: 'Gênero', opcoes: _generos, selecionado: _genero, onSelect: (v) => setState(() => _genero = v)),
 529:             const SizedBox(height: 6),
 530:             _linhaFiltro(titulo: 'Ano', opcoes: _anos, selecionado: _ano, onSelect: (v) => setState(() => _ano = v)),
 531:             const SizedBox(height: 6),
 532:             _linhaFiltro(titulo: 'País', opcoes: _paises, selecionado: _pais, onSelect: (v) => setState(() => _pais = v)),
 533:             const SizedBox(height: 6),
 534:             _linhaFiltro(titulo: 'Áudio', opcoes: _audios, selecionado: _audio, onSelect: (v) => setState(() => _audio = v)),
 535:             const SizedBox(height: 16),
 536:             Expanded(
 537:               child: lista.isEmpty
 538:                   ? const Center(child: Text('Nenhum título encontrado com essa combinação de filtros.', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 16)))
 539:                   : GridView.builder(
 540:                       gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithMaxCrossAxisExtent(maxCrossAxisExtent: 168, childAspectRatio: 0.62, crossAxisSpacing: 14, mainAxisSpacing: 14),
 541:                       itemCount: lista.length,
 542:                       itemBuilder: (_, idx) => TvPosterCard(obra: lista[idx], onTap: () => widget.onAbrirObra(lista[idx])),
 543:                     ),
 544:             ),
 545:           ],
 546:         ),
 547:       ),
 548:     );
 549:   }
 550: }
 551: 
 552: class TvHistoricoFavoritosScreen extends StatefulWidget {
 553:   final List<ObraAgrupada> catalogo;
 554:   final void Function(ObraAgrupada) onAbrirObra;
 555: 
 556:   const TvHistoricoFavoritosScreen({
 557:     super.key,
 558:     required this.catalogo,
 559:     required this.onAbrirObra,
 560:   });
 561: 
 562:   @override
 563:   State<TvHistoricoFavoritosScreen> createState() => _TvHistoricoFavoritosScreenState();
 564: }
 565: 
 566: class _TvHistoricoFavoritosScreenState extends State<TvHistoricoFavoritosScreen> {
 567:   int _abaLateral = 0;
 568:   bool _somenteSeries = false;
 569:   bool _modoEdicao = false;
 570:   final Set<String> _selecionados = {};
 571:   Map<String, Map<String, dynamic>> _historicoMap = {};
 572:   Set<String> _favoritosSet = {};
 573: 
 574:   @override
 575:   void initState() {
 576:     super.initState();
 577:     _carregarDados();
 578:   }
 579: 
 580:   Future<void> _carregarDados() async {
 581:     final hist = await SupabaseService.carregarProgressoAssistido();
 582:     final favs = await SupabaseService.carregarFavoritos();
 583:     if (mounted) {
 584:       setState(() {
 585:         _historicoMap = hist;
 586:         _favoritosSet = favs;
 587:       });
 588:     }
 589:   }
 590: 
 591:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _itensAtuais {
 592:     final base = _abaLateral == 0
 593:         ? widget.catalogo.where((o) => _historicoMap.containsKey(o.chaveGrupo)).toList()
 594:         : widget.catalogo.where((o) => _favoritosSet.contains(o.chaveGrupo)).toList();
 595:     return base.where((o) => _somenteSeries ? o.isSerie : !o.isSerie).toList();
 596:   }
 597: 
 598:   Future<void> _excluirSelecionados() async {
 599:     if (_selecionados.isEmpty) return;
 600:     if (_abaLateral == 0) {
 601:       await SupabaseService.removerDoHistorico(_selecionados.toList());
 602:     } else {
 603:       for (final ch in _selecionados) {
 604:         await SupabaseService.alternarFavorito(ch);
 605:       }
 606:     }
 607:     setState(() {
 608:       _selecionados.clear();
 609:       _modoEdicao = false;
 610:     });
 611:     await _carregarDados();
 612:   }
 613: 
 614:   Future<void> _limparTudo() async {
 615:     final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
 616:       context: context,
 617:       builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
 618:         backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF161B24),
 619:         title: Text(_abaLateral == 0 ? 'Limpar Histórico?' : 'Limpar Favoritos?'),
 620:         content: const Text('Tem certeza que deseja remover todos os itens desta lista?'),
 621:         actions: [
 622:           TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: const Text('Cancelar')),
 623:           ElevatedButton(
 624:             style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.redAccent),
 625:             onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
 626:             child: const Text('Excluir tudo'),
 627:           ),
 628:         ],
 629:       ),
 630:     );
 631:     if (ok == true) {
 632:       if (_abaLateral == 0) {
 633:         await SupabaseService.limparTodoHistorico();
 634:       } else {
 635:         for (final ch in _favoritosSet.toList()) {
 636:           await SupabaseService.alternarFavorito(ch);
 637:         }
 638:       }
 639:       setState(() {
 640:         _selecionados.clear();
 641:         _modoEdicao = false;
 642:       });
 643:       await _carregarDados();
 644:     }
 645:   }
 646: 
 647:   @override
 648:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 649:     final baseAtual = _abaLateral == 0
 650:         ? widget.catalogo.where((o) => _historicoMap.containsKey(o.chaveGrupo)).toList()
 651:         : widget.catalogo.where((o) => _favoritosSet.contains(o.chaveGrupo)).toList();
 652:     final totalFilmes = baseAtual.where((o) => !o.isSerie).length;
 653:     final totalSeries = baseAtual.where((o) => o.isSerie).length;
 654:     final exibidos = _itensAtuais;
 655: 
 656:     return Scaffold(
 657:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0E141B),
 658:       appBar: AppBar(
 659:         backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
 660:         title: Text(_abaLateral == 0 ? 'Histórico' : 'Meus Favoritos'),
 661:       ),
 662:       body: Row(
 663:         children: [
 664:           Container(
 665:             width: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width < 420 ? 96 : (MediaQuery.of(context).size.width < 700 ? 112 : 230),
 666:             padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 20),
 667:             color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.24),
 668:             child: Column(
 669:               crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
 670:               children: [
 671:                 _itemMenuLateral(
 672:                   titulo: 'Histórico',
 673:                   ativo: _abaLateral == 0,
 674:                   onTap: () => setState(() {
 675:                     _abaLateral = 0;
 676:                     _modoEdicao = false;
 677:                     _selecionados.clear();
 678:                   }),
 679:                 ),
 680:                 const SizedBox(height: 8),

 685:                     _abaLateral = 1;
 686:                     _modoEdicao = false;
 687:                     _selecionados.clear();
 688:                   }),
 689:                 ),
 690:               ],
 691:             ),
 692:           ),
 693:           Expanded(
 694:             child: Padding(

 696:               child: Column(

 698:                 children: [
 699:                   Row(
 700:                     children: [
 701:                       _pilulaTipo(label: 'Filmes', ativo: !_somenteSeries, onTap: () => setState(() => _somenteSeries = false)),
 702:                       const SizedBox(width: 12),
 703:                       _pilulaTipo(label: 'Séries', ativo: _somenteSeries, onTap: () => setState(() => _somenteSeries = true)),

 713:                               onPressed: () => setState(() {

 715:                                 _selecionados.clear();
 716:                               }),
 717:                             ),
 718:                             IconButton(

 722:                             ),
 723:                           ],

 829: class TvTop100RankingScreen extends StatefulWidget {
 830:   final String fonte;
 831:   final String categoriaInicial;
 832:   final List<ObraAgrupada> catalogo;
 833:   final void Function(ObraAgrupada) onAbrirObra;
 834: 
 835:   const TvTop100RankingScreen({
 836:     super.key,
 837:     required this.fonte,
 838:     this.categoriaInicial = 'Filmes',
 839:     required this.catalogo,
 840:     required this.onAbrirObra,
 841:   });
 842: 
 843:   @override
 844:   State<TvTop100RankingScreen> createState() => _TvTop100RankingScreenState();
 845: }
 846: 
 847: class _TvTop100RankingScreenState extends State<TvTop100RankingScreen> {
 848:   late String _categoria;
 849:   bool _carregando = true;
 850:   List<ObraAgrupada> _lista = [];
 851:   static const List<String> _cats = ['Filmes', 'Séries', 'Kids', 'Anime'];
 852: 
 853:   @override
 854:   void initState() {
 855:     super.initState();
 856:     _categoria = widget.categoriaInicial;
 857:     _carregar();
 858:   }
 859: 
 860:   Future<void> _carregar() async {
 861:     setState(() => _carregando = true);
 862:     final res = await SupabaseService.carregarTop100Ranking(
 863:       fonte: widget.fonte,
 864:       categoria: _categoria,
 865:       catalogoLocal: widget.catalogo,
 866:     );
 867:     if (mounted) {
 868:       setState(() {
 869:         _lista = res;
 870:         _carregando = false;
 871:       });
 872:     }
 873:   }
 874: 
 875:   @override
 876:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 877:     final tituloBase = widget.fonte == 'imdb' ? 'Top 100 IMDb' : 'Top 100 TMDB';
 878:     return Scaffold(
 879:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0B0C10),
 880:       appBar: AppBar(
 881:         backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
 882:         title: Text('$tituloBase • $_categoria', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
 883:       ),
 884:       body: Column(
 885:         children: [
 886:           SizedBox(
 887:             height: 44,
 888:             child: ListView.separated(
 889:               padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
 890:               scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
 891:               itemCount: _cats.length,
 892:               separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 10),
 893:               itemBuilder: (_, idx) {
 894:                 final c = _cats[idx];
 895:                 final ativo = c == _categoria;
 896:                 return ChoiceChip(
 897:                   label: Text(c),
 898:                   selected: ativo,
 899:                   selectedColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E),
 900:                   labelStyle: TextStyle(color: ativo ? Colors.black : Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
 901:                   onSelected: (_) {
 902:                     setState(() => _categoria = c);
 903:                     _carregar();
 904:                   },
 905:                 );
 906:               },
 907:             ),
 908:           ),
 909:           const SizedBox(height: 14),
 910:           Expanded(
 911:             child: _carregando
 912:                 ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Color(0xFFF5B82E)))
 913:                 : GridView.builder(
 914:                     padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 4, 24, 20),
 915:                     gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithMaxCrossAxisExtent(
 916:                       maxCrossAxisExtent: 168,
 917:                       childAspectRatio: 0.62,
 918:                       crossAxisSpacing: 14,
 919:                       mainAxisSpacing: 14,
 920:                     ),
 921:                     itemCount: _lista.length,
 922:                     itemBuilder: (_, idx) => TvPosterCard(
 923:                       obra: _lista[idx],
 924:                       rankNumero: idx + 1,
 925:                       onTap: () => widget.onAbrirObra(_lista[idx]),
 926:                     ),
 927:                   ),
 928:           ),
 929:         ],
 930:       ),
 931:     );
 932:   }
 933: }

 935: class TvSubcategoriaScreen extends StatefulWidget {
 936:   final String titulo;

 938:   final List<String> subfiltros;
 939:   final String? filtroInicial;

 941:   final VoidCallback onAbrirBusca;
 942:   final VoidCallback onAbrirFiltro;
 943: 
 944:   const TvSubcategoriaScreen({
 945:     super.key,
 946:     required this.titulo,
 947:     required this.itens,
 948:     required this.subfiltros,
 949:     this.filtroInicial,
 950:     required this.onAbrirObra,
 951:     required this.onAbrirBusca,
 952:     required this.onAbrirFiltro,
 953:   });
 954: 
 955:   @override
 956:   State<TvSubcategoriaScreen> createState() => _TvSubcategoriaScreenState();
 957: }
 958: 
 959: class _TvSubcategoriaScreenState extends State<TvSubcategoriaScreen> {
 960:   bool _menuAberto = true;
 961:   late String _selecionado;
 962: 
 963:   @override
 964:   void initState() {
 965:     super.initState();
 966:     _selecionado = widget.filtroInicial ?? widget.subfiltros.first;
 967:   }
 968: 
 969:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _filtrados {
 970:     if (_selecionado == 'Todos' || _selecionado == 'Tudo') return widget.itens;
 971:     final busca = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(_selecionado);
 972:     return widget.itens.where((o) {
 973:       final g = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 974:       final t = o.tags.map((e) => SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(e)).toList();
 975:       final tit = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.titulo);
 976:       return g.contains(busca) || t.any((x) => x.contains(busca)) || tit.contains(busca);
 977:     }).toList();
 978:   }
 979: 
 980:   @override
 981:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 982:     final lista = _filtrados;
 983:     return Scaffold(
 984:       backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0B0C10),
 985:       appBar: AppBar(
 986:         backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
 987:         leading: IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back), onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context)),
 988:         title: Row(
 989:           children: [
 990:             IconButton(
 991:               tooltip: _menuAberto ? 'Recolher menu' : 'Expandir menu',
 992:               icon: Icon(_menuAberto ? Icons.menu_open : Icons.menu),
 993:               onPressed: () => setState(() => _menuAberto = !_menuAberto),
 994:             ),
 995:             const SizedBox(width: 6),
 996:             Text('${widget.titulo} • $_selecionado', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
 997:           ],
 998:         ),
 999:         actions: [
1000:           PerfilChipTopButton(onTap: () => PerfisSelectionOverlay.abrir(context, onPerfilSelecionado: () { if (mounted) setState(() {}); })), const SizedBox(width: 6), MaxMicButton(onPressed: () => MaxAssistantModal.abrir(context)), const SizedBox(width: 6), TopIconeComTooltip(icon: Icons.playlist_add_rounded, label: 'Pedidos', onPressed: () => PedidosScreenModal.abrir(context)), const SizedBox(width: 6), TopIconeComTooltip(icon: Icons.search, label: 'Buscar', onPressed: widget.onAbrirBusca),
1001:           const SizedBox(width: 10),
1002:           TopIconeComTooltip(icon: Icons.filter_alt_outlined, label: 'Filtro', onPressed: widget.onAbrirFiltro),
1003:           const SizedBox(width: 20),
1004:         ],
1005:       ),
1006:       body: Row(
1007:         children: [
1008:           if (_menuAberto)
1009:             Container(
1010:               width: 210,
1011:               color: const Color(0xFF11141C),
1012:               child: ListView.builder(
1013:                 padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
1014:                 itemCount: widget.subfiltros.length,
1015:                 itemBuilder: (_, idx) {
1016:                   final f = widget.subfiltros[idx];
1017:                   final ativo = f == _selecionado;
1018:                   return ListTile(
1019:                     selected: ativo,
1020:                     selectedTileColor: const Color(0xFFF5B82E).withOpacity(0.18),
1021:                     title: Text(
1022:                       f,
1023:                       style: TextStyle(color: ativo ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.white70, fontWeight: ativo ? FontWeight.w800 : FontWeight.w500),
1024:                     ),
1025:                     onTap: () => setState(() => _selecionado = f),
1026:                   );
1027:                 },
1028:               ),
1029:             ),
1030:           Expanded(
1031:             child: lista.isEmpty
1032:                 ? const Center(child: Text('Nenhum título nesta subcategoria.', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)))
1033:                 : GridView.builder(
1034:                     padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
1035:                     gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithMaxCrossAxisExtent(
1036:                       maxCrossAxisExtent: 168,
1037:                       childAspectRatio: 0.62,
1038:                       crossAxisSpacing: 14,
1039:                       mainAxisSpacing: 14,
1040:                     ),
1041:                     itemCount: lista.length,
1042:                     itemBuilder: (_, idx) => TvPosterCard(obra: lista[idx], onTap: () => widget.onAbrirObra(lista[idx])),
1043:                   ),
1044:           ),
1045:         ],
1046:       ),
1047:     );
1048:   }
1049: }

  43:   @override
  44:   void initState() {
  45:     super.initState();
  46:     _carregarProgresso();
  47:     Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 3), () {
  48:       if (mounted) AppUpdaterService.verificarAtualizacao(context, silencioso: true);
  49:     });
  50:     _autoPlayTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 6), (_) {
  51:       if (!mounted || _abaIndex != 0) return;
  52:       if (_bannerFilmesCtrl.hasClients) {
  53:         final nextF = (_idxBannerFilme + 1) % 4;
  54:         _bannerFilmesCtrl.animateToPage(nextF, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500), curve: Curves.easeInOut);
  55:       }
  56:       if (_bannerSeriesCtrl.hasClients) {
  57:         final nextS = (_idxBannerSerie + 1) % 4;
  58:         _bannerSeriesCtrl.animateToPage(nextS, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500), curve: Curves.easeInOut);
  59:       }
  60:     });
  61:   }
  62: 
  63:   Future<void> _carregarProgresso() async {
  64:     final p = await SupabaseService.carregarProgressoAssistido();
  65:     if (mounted) setState(() => _progressoMap = p);
  66:   }
  67: 
  68:   @override
  69:   void dispose() {
  70:     _autoPlayTimer?.cancel();
  71:     _bannerFilmesCtrl.dispose();
  72:     _bannerSeriesCtrl.dispose();
  73:     super.dispose();
  74:   }
  75: 
  76:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _filmes => widget.catalogo
  77:       .where((o) => o.categoria.toLowerCase().contains('filme') && !o.isSerie)
  78:       .toList();
  79: 
  80:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _series =>
  81:       widget.catalogo.where((o) => o.isSerie).toList();
  82: 
  83:   bool _ehAnime(ObraAgrupada o) {
  84:     final c = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.categoria);
  85:     final g = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
  86:     final t = o.tags.map((e) => SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(e)).toList();
  87:     if (c.contains('anime') || g.contains('anime') || t.contains('anime')) return true;
  88:     return CatalogAuditService.ehAnime(
  89:       titulo: o.titulo,
  90:       generos: o.genero.split(','),
  91:       paisOrigem: o.pais,
  92:       sinopse: o.sinopse,
  93:     );
  94:   }
  95: 
  96:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _kids => widget.catalogo.where((o) {
  97:         if (_ehAnime(o)) return false;
  98:         final c = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.categoria);
  99:         final g = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 100:         final t = o.tags.map((e) => SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(e)).toList();
 101:         return c.contains('infantil') || c.contains('kids') || g.contains('infantil') || g.contains('animacao') || t.contains('kids');
 102:       }).toList();
 103: 
 104:   List<ObraAgrupada> get _animes => widget.catalogo.where(_ehAnime).toList();
 105: 
 106:   List<ObraAgrupada> _filtrarTagOuGenero(List<ObraAgrupada> base, String termo) {
 107:     final q = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(termo);
 108:     return base.where((o) {
 109:       final g = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 110:       final c = SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(o.categoria);
 111:       final t = o.tags.map((e) => SupabaseService.normalizarTexto(e)).toList();
 112:       return g.contains(q) || c.contains(q) || t.any((x) => x.contains(q));
 113:     }).toList();
 114:   }

 168:           onAbrirFiltro: () => _abrirFiltro(titulo),
 169:         ),
 170:       ),
 171:     );

 300:   Widget _buildAbaInicio() {
 301:     final filmesDestaque = (_filmes.where((o) => o.emDestaque || o.tags.any((t) => t.toLowerCase().contains('banner'))).toList()
 302:           ..sort((a, b) => a.ordemDestaque.compareTo(b.ordemDestaque)))
 303:         .take(4)
 304:         .toList();
 305:     if (filmesDestaque.length < 4) {
 306:       for (final f in _filmes) {
 307:         if (!filmesDestaque.contains(f)) filmesDestaque.add(f);
 308:         if (filmesDestaque.length == 4) break;
 309:       }
 310:     }
 311: 
 312:     final seriesDestaque = (_series.where((o) => o.emDestaque || o.tags.any((t) => t.toLowerCase().contains('banner'))).toList()
 313:           ..sort((a, b) => a.ordemDestaque.compareTo(b.ordemDestaque)))
 314:         .take(4)
 315:         .toList();
 316:     if (seriesDestaque.length < 4) {
 317:       for (final s in _series) {
 318:         if (!seriesDestaque.contains(s)) seriesDestaque.add(s);
 319:         if (seriesDestaque.length == 4) break;
 320:       }
 321:     }
 322: 
 323:     final continuar = widget.catalogo.where((o) => _progressoMap.containsKey(o.chaveGrupo)).toList();
 324:     final top10 = List<ObraAgrupada>.from(widget.catalogo)
 325:       ..sort((a, b) => b.scorePopularidade.compareTo(a.scorePopularidade));
 326: 
 327:     return ListView(
 328:       cacheExtent: 320,
 329:       padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(26, 8, 26, 36),
 330:       children: [
 331:         // Dois Banners Rotativos Lado a Lado (4 Filmes + 4 Séries)
 332:         SizedBox(
 333:           height: 255,
 334:           child: Row(
 335:             children: [
 336:               Expanded(
 337:                 child: _buildCarrosselHero(
 338:                   tituloSecao: 'Filmes em Destaque',
 339:                   itens: filmesDestaque,
 340:                   controller: _bannerFilmesCtrl,
 341:                   paginaAtual: _idxBannerFilme,
 342:                   onPageChanged: (i) => setState(() => _idxBannerFilme = i),
 343:                 ),
 344:               ),
 345:               const SizedBox(width: 18),
 346:               Expanded(
 347:                 child: _buildCarrosselHero(
 348:                   tituloSecao: 'Séries em Destaque',
 349:                   itens: seriesDestaque,
 350:                   controller: _bannerSeriesCtrl,
 351:                   paginaAtual: _idxBannerSerie,
 352:                   onPageChanged: (i) => setState(() => _idxBannerSerie = i),
 353:                 ),
 354:               ),
 355:             ],
 356:           ),
 357:         ),
 358:         const SizedBox(height: 22),
 359:         if (continuar.isNotEmpty)
 360:           _buildPrateleira(
 361:             titulo: 'Continuar Assistindo',
 362:             itens: continuar,
 363:             mostrarProgresso: true,
 364:           ),
 365:         _buildPrateleira(
 366:           titulo: 'Top 10 em Alta',
 367:           itens: top10.take(10).toList(),
 368:           numerado: true,
 369:         ),
 370:         _buildPrateleira(
 371:           titulo: 'Sports',
 372:           itens: _filtrarTagOuGenero(widget.catalogo, 'Sport'),
 373:         ),
 374:         _buildPrateleira(
 375:           titulo: 'Livestream',
 376:           itens: _filtrarTagOuGenero(widget.catalogo, 'TV'),
 377:         ),
 378:         _buildPrateleira(
 379:           titulo: 'No Cinema',
 380:           itens: _filtrarTagOuGenero(widget.catalogo, 'Cinema'),
 381:         ),
 382:         _buildPrateleira(
 383:           titulo: 'Coleções',
 384:           itens: _filtrarTagOuGenero(widget.catalogo, 'Cole'),
 385:         ),
 386:         _buildPrateleira(
 387:           titulo: 'Ação',
 388:           itens: _filtrarTagOuGenero(widget.catalogo, 'Ação'),
 389:         ),
 390:         _buildPrateleira(
 391:           titulo: 'Mistério',
 392:           itens: _filtrarTagOuGenero(widget.catalogo, 'Mistério'),
 393:         ),
 394:         _buildPrateleira(
 395:           titulo: 'Suspense',
 396:           itens: _filtrarTagOuGenero(widget.catalogo, 'Suspense'),
 397:         ),
 398:         _buildPrateleira(
 399:           titulo: 'Netflix',
 400:           itens: _filtrarTagOuGenero(widget.catalogo, 'Netflix'),
 401:         ),
 402:         _buildPrateleira(
 403:           titulo: 'Disney+',
 404:           itens: _filtrarTagOuGenero(widget.catalogo, 'Disney'),
 405:         ),
 406:         _buildPrateleira(
 407:           titulo: 'Globoplay',
 408:           itens: _filtrarTagOuGenero(widget.catalogo, 'Globoplay'),
 409:         ),
 410:       ],
 411:     );
