  98:   static String? _extrairLogoM3u(String texto) {
  99:     final m = RegExp(r'tvg-logo\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"', caseSensitive: false)
 100:         .firstMatch(texto);
 101:     return (m != null && m.group(1)!.startsWith('http'))
 102:         ? m.group(1)!.trim()
 103:         : null;
 104:   }
 105: 
 106:   static String _limparExtinf(String texto) {
 107:     var s = texto.trim();
 108:     if (s.toUpperCase().startsWith('#EXTINF')) {
 109:       final cut = s.lastIndexOf('"') > s.lastIndexOf(',')
 110:           ? s.lastIndexOf('"')
 111:           : s.lastIndexOf(',');
 112:       if (cut != -1 && cut + 1 < s.length) s = s.substring(cut + 1).trim();
 113:     }
 114:     return s;
 115:   }
 116: 
 117:   static Map<String, int>? _extrairTempEp(String titulo) {
 118:     for (final rx in [
 119:       RegExp(r'(?:S|T)\s*(\d{1,2})\s*[\|\-:\.\s]*E(?:P)?\s*(\d{1,3})',
 120:           caseSensitive: false),
 121:       RegExp(r'(\d{1,2})\s*[xX]\s*(\d{1,3})'),
 122:       RegExp(r'TEMPORADA\s*(\d{1,2}).*?EP(?:IS[OÓ]DIO)?\s*(\d{1,3})',
 123:           caseSensitive: false),
 124:     ]) {
 125:       final m = rx.firstMatch(titulo);
 126:       if (m != null) {
 127:         return {'t': int.parse(m.group(1)!), 'e': int.parse(m.group(2)!)};
 128:       }
 129:     }
 130:     final epOnly = RegExp(
 131:             r'(?:^|[\s\|\-\[\(])EP(?:IS[OÓ]DIO)?\s*0*(\d{1,3})(?:$|[\s\|\-\]\)])',
 132:             caseSensitive: false)
 133:         .firstMatch(titulo);
 134:     if (epOnly != null) return {'t': 1, 'e': int.parse(epOnly.group(1)!)};
 135:     return null;
 136:   }
 137: 
 138:   static String _limparNomeObra(String titulo) {
 139:     var s = _limparExtinf(titulo)
 140:         .replaceAll(
 141:             RegExp(
 142:                 r'[\|\-\[\(]?\s*(?:S|T)\s*\d{1,2}\s*[\|\-:\.\s]*E(?:P)?\s*\d{1,3}.*$',
 143:                 caseSensitive: false),
 144:             '')
 145:         .replaceAll(
 146:             RegExp(r'[\|\-\[\(]?\s*\d{1,2}\s*[xX]\s*\d{1,3}.*$',
 147:                 caseSensitive: false),
 148:             '')
 149:         .replaceAll(
 150:             RegExp(r'[\|\-\[\(]?\s*TEMPORADA\s*\d{1,2}.*$',
 151:                 caseSensitive: false),
 152:             '')
 153:         .replaceAll(
 154:             RegExp(r'[\|\-\[\(]?\s*EP(?:IS[OÓ]DIO)?\s*\d{1,3}.*$',
 155:                 caseSensitive: false),
 156:             '')
 157:         .replaceAll(
 158:             RegExp(
 159:                 r'\s*[\(\[]?(?:dublado|legendado|dual|nacional|hd|fhd|4k|1080p|720p)[\)\]]',
 160:                 caseSensitive: false),
 161:             '')
 162:         .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[\|\-:\s]+$'), '')
 163:         .trim();
 164:     return s.isEmpty ? _limparExtinf(titulo) : s;
 165:   }
 166: 
 167:   static String normalizarTexto(String texto) => texto
 168:       .toLowerCase()
 169:       .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[áàâãä]'), 'a')
 170:       .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[éèêë]'), 'e')
 171:       .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[íìîï]'), 'i')
 172:       .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[óòôõö]'), 'o')
 173:       .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[úùûü]'), 'u')
 174:       .replaceAll('ç', 'c');
 175: 
 176:   static String _normalizarChave(String texto) =>
 177:       normalizarTexto(_limparNomeObra(texto)).replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z0-9]'), '');
 178: 
 179:   static String _normalizarUrlImagem(String url) {
 180:     var u = url.trim();
 181:     if (u.startsWith('http://image.tmdb.org') ||
 182:         u.startsWith('http://i.imgur.com')) {
 183:       u = u.replaceFirst('http://', 'https://');
 184:     }
 185:     return u;
 186:   }
 187: 
 188:   static bool _urlEstaAtiva(String url) {
 189:     final u = url.toLowerCase();
 190:     for (final m in [
 191:       'staniptv',
 192:       'ntcdn.us',
 193:       'vstreaming.net',
 194:       'popfilmeshd.net',
 195:       'themediafire.club',
 196:       'cdnv12.ec.cx',
 197:       'cdnv18.ec.cx',
 198:       '0.0.0.0',
 199:       'lh3.googleusercontent.com',
 200:       'dailymotion.com/sec('
 201:     ]) {
 202:       if (u.contains(m)) return false;
 203:     }
 204:     return true;
 205:   }
 206: 
 207:   static List<ObraAgrupada> _agruparDados(List<dynamic> dados) {
 208:     final Map<String, List<Map<String, dynamic>>> grupos = {};
 209:     final Set<String> idsVistos = {};
 210: 
 211:     for (final item in dados) {
 212:       if (item is! Map) continue;
 213:       final map = Map<String, dynamic>.from(item);
 214:       final id = (map['id'] ?? '').toString();
 215:       if (id.isNotEmpty && !idsVistos.add(id)) continue;
 216:       final url = (map['url_stream'] ?? '').toString().trim();
 217:       if (url.isEmpty || !_urlEstaAtiva(url)) continue;
 218: 
 219:       final tituloBruto = (map['titulo'] ?? 'Sem Título').toString().trim();
 220:       var capa = _normalizarUrlImagem((map['capa_url'] ?? '').toString());
 221:       if (capa.isEmpty) {
 222:         final logo = _extrairLogoM3u(tituloBruto);
 223:         if (logo != null) capa = _normalizarUrlImagem(logo);
 224:       }
 225:       map['capa_url'] = capa;
 226:       map['banner_url'] =
 227:           _normalizarUrlImagem((map['banner_url'] ?? '').toString());
 228:       final tituloLimpo = _limparExtinf(tituloBruto);
 229:       map['titulo'] = tituloLimpo;
 230: 
 231:       final te = _extrairTempEp(tituloLimpo);
 232:       final catOrig = (map['categoria'] ?? 'Filme').toString().trim();
 233:       final isSerie = te != null ||
 234:           map['episodio'] != null ||
 235:           catOrig.toLowerCase().contains('série') ||
 236:           catOrig.toLowerCase().contains('serie');
 237:       if (te != null) {
 238:         map['temporada'] = te['t'];
 239:         map['episodio'] = te['e'];
 240:       }
 241:       if (isSerie &&
 242:           !catOrig.toLowerCase().contains('anime') &&
 243:           !catOrig.toLowerCase().contains('infantil') &&
 244:           !catOrig.toLowerCase().contains('kids')) {
 245:         map['categoria'] = 'Série';
 246:       }
 247: 
 248:       final serieNome = (map['serie_nome'] ?? '').toString().trim();
 249:       final norm = _normalizarChave(
 250:           serieNome.isNotEmpty ? _limparNomeObra(serieNome) : _limparNomeObra(tituloLimpo));
 251:       grupos.putIfAbsent(isSerie ? 'serie_$norm' : 'filme_$norm', () => []).add(map);
 252:     }
 253: 
 254:     final List<ObraAgrupada> obras = [];
 255:     grupos.forEach((chave, lista) {
 256:       lista.sort((a, b) => ((b['capa_url'] ?? '').toString().isNotEmpty ? 1 : 0)
 257:           .compareTo((a['capa_url'] ?? '').toString().isNotEmpty ? 1 : 0));
 258:       final p = lista.first;
 259:       final isSerie = chave.startsWith('serie_');
 260:       final catOrig = (p['categoria'] ?? 'Filme').toString().trim();
 261:       final cat = isSerie &&
 262:               !catOrig.toLowerCase().contains('anime') &&
 263:               !catOrig.toLowerCase().contains('infantil') &&
 264:               !catOrig.toLowerCase().contains('kids')
 265:           ? 'Série'
 266:           : catOrig;
 267:       final serieNome = (p['serie_nome'] ?? '').toString().trim();
 268:       final tituloFinal = serieNome.isNotEmpty
 269:           ? _limparNomeObra(serieNome)
 270:           : _limparNomeObra((p['titulo'] ?? '').toString());
 271: 
 272:       final Set<String> tagsSet = {};
 273:       int viewsTotal = 0;
 274:       double maiorNota = 0.0;
 275:       bool algumDestaque = false;
 276:       int menorOrdem = 999;
 277: 
 278:       for (final row in lista) {
 279:         if (row['tags'] is List) {
 280:           for (final t in (row['tags'] as List)) {
 281:             if (t != null && t.toString().trim().isNotEmpty) {
 282:               tagsSet.add(t.toString().trim());
 283:             }
 284:           }
 285:         }
 286:         if (row['em_destaque'] == true) {
 287:           algumDestaque = true;
 288:           tagsSet.add('Banner Principal');
 289:         }
 290:         final ord =
 291:             row['ordem_destaque'] is int ? row['ordem_destaque'] as int : 999;
 292:         if (ord < menorOrdem) menorOrdem = ord;
 293:         viewsTotal +=
 294:             (row['visualizacoes'] is num) ? (row['visualizacoes'] as num).toInt() : 0;
 295:         final nt =
 296:             (row['nota_imdb'] is num) ? (row['nota_imdb'] as num).toDouble() : 0.0;
 297:         if (nt > maiorNota) maiorNota = nt;
 298:       }
 299: 
 300:       obras.add(ObraAgrupada(
 301:         chaveGrupo: chave,
 302:         tmdbId: p['tmdb_id'] is int
 303:             ? p['tmdb_id']
 304:             : int.tryParse('${p['tmdb_id'] ?? ''}'),
 305:         titulo: tituloFinal,
 306:         tituloOriginal: (p['titulo_original'] ?? '').toString(),
 307:         categoria: cat,
 308:         genero: (p['genero'] ?? '').toString(),
 309:         sinopse: (p['sinopse'] ?? '').toString(),
 310:         capaUrl: (p['capa_url'] ?? '').toString(),
 311:         bannerUrl: (p['banner_url'] ?? '').toString(),
 312:         ano: (p['ano'] ?? '').toString(),
 313:         pais: (p['pais'] ?? '').toString(),
 314:         diretor: (p['diretor'] ?? '').toString(),
 315:         elenco: (p['elenco'] ?? '').toString(),
 316:         aberturaSegundos: p['abertura_segundos'] is int
 317:             ? p['abertura_segundos']
 318:             : int.tryParse('${p['abertura_segundos'] ?? 85}') ?? 85,
 319:         emDestaque: algumDestaque,
 320:         ordemDestaque: menorOrdem,
 321:         tags: tagsSet.toList(),
 322:         notaImdb: maiorNota,
 323:         visualizacoes: viewsTotal,
 324:         fontes: lista.map<FonteMidia>((e) => FonteMidia.fromJson(e)).toList(),
 325:       ));
 326:     });
 327: 
 328:     obras.sort((a, b) =>
 329:         (a.capaUrl.isNotEmpty ? 0 : 1).compareTo(b.capaUrl.isNotEmpty ? 0 : 1));
 330:     return obras;
 331:   }
 332: 
 333:   static Future<List<dynamic>> _fetchLote(
 334:       String key, String filtroQuery, int limite) async {
 335:     const cols =
 336:         'id,tmdb_id,titulo,serie_nome,categoria,genero,sinopse,capa_url,banner_url,ano,temporada,episodio,url_stream,url_stream_backup,headers_customizados,formato,tipo_audio,resolucao,em_destaque,ordem_destaque,tags,nota_imdb,visualizacoes';
 337:     const filtroAtivos =
 338:         '&url_stream=not.ilike.*STANIPTV*&url_stream=not.ilike.*ntcdn.us*&url_stream=not.ilike.*vstreaming.net*&url_stream=not.ilike.*popfilmeshd.net*&url_stream=not.ilike.*themediafire.club*&url_stream=not.ilike.*cdnv12.ec.cx*&url_stream=not.ilike.*cdnv18.ec.cx*&url_stream=not.ilike.*0.0.0.0*&url_stream=not.ilike.*lh3.googleusercontent.com*&url_stream=not.ilike.*dailymotion.com/sec(';
 339:     final uri = Uri.parse(
 340:         '$baseUrl/rest/v1/catalogo_midia?select=$cols$filtroAtivos$filtroQuery&order=ordem_destaque.asc,criado_em.desc');
 341:     final resp = await http.get(uri, headers: {
 342:       'apikey': key,
 343:       'Authorization': 'Bearer $key',
 344:       'Content-Type': 'application/json',
 345:       'Range': '0-${limite - 1}',
 346:     }).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 12));
 347:     return (resp.statusCode == 200 || resp.statusCode == 206)
 348:         ? jsonDecode(utf8.decode(resp.bodyBytes)) as List<dynamic>
 349:         : [];
 350:   }
 351: 
 352:   static Future<List<ObraAgrupada>> carregarCatalogoAgrupado(
 353:       {bool forcarRefresh = false}) async {
 354:     if (!forcarRefresh && _memoriaRapida != null && _memoriaRapida!.isNotEmpty) {
 355:       return _memoriaRapida!;
 356:     }
 357:     final key = await getAnonKey();
 358:     final res = await Future.wait([
 359:       _fetchLote(key, '&categoria=ilike.*Filme*', 1200),
 360:       _fetchLote(key, '&categoria=ilike.*S*rie*', 2000),
 361:       _fetchLote(key, '&or=(categoria.ilike.*Anime*,genero.ilike.*Anime*)', 800),
 362:       _fetchLote(
 363:           key,
 364:           '&or=(categoria.ilike.*Infantil*,categoria.ilike.*Kids*,genero.ilike.*Infantil*,genero.ilike.*Anima*)',
 365:           800),
 366:       _fetchLote(key, '&or=(categoria.ilike.*TV*,categoria.ilike.*Sport*,categoria.ilike.*Live*)', 400),
 367:     ]);
 368:     final combinado = [...res[0], ...res[1], ...res[2], ...res[3], ...res[4]];
 369:     if (combinado.isEmpty) {
 370:       combinado.addAll(await _fetchLote(key, '', 2000));
 371:     }
 372:     _memoriaRapida = _agruparDados(combinado);
 373:     return _memoriaRapida!;
 374:   }
 375: 
 376:   static Future<void> registrarVisualizacao(String id) async {
 377:     try {
 378:       final key = await getAnonKey();
 379:       await http.post(
 380:         Uri.parse('$baseUrl/rest/v1/rpc/incrementar_visualizacao'),
 381:         headers: {
 382:           'apikey': key,
 383:           'Authorization': 'Bearer $key',
 384:           'Content-Type': 'application/json',
 385:         },
 386:         body: jsonEncode({'media_id': id}),
 387:       );
 388:     } catch (_) {}
 389:   }
 390: 
 391:   // =========================================================================
 392:   // TMDB: METADADOS COMPLETOS (DIRETOR, ELENCO, PAÍS, TÍTULO ORIGINAL)
 393:   // =========================================================================
 394:   static Future<ObraAgrupada> enriquecerComTmdb(ObraAgrupada obra) async {
 395:     if (obra.diretor.isNotEmpty &&
 396:         obra.elenco.isNotEmpty &&
 397:         obra.pais.isNotEmpty) {
 398:       return obra;
 399:     }
 400:     if (_cacheCreditosTmdb.containsKey(obra.chaveGrupo)) {
 401:       final c = _cacheCreditosTmdb[obra.chaveGrupo]!;
 402:       return obra.copyWith(
 403:         tituloOriginal: c['tituloOriginal'],
 404:         pais: c['pais'],
 405:         diretor: c['diretor'],
 406:         elenco: c['elenco'],
 407:         sinopse: c['sinopse'],
 408:         notaImdb: c['nota'],
 409:       );
 410:     }
 411: 
 412:     try {
 413:       final tipo = obra.isSerie ? 'tv' : 'movie';
 414:       int? idTmdb = obra.tmdbId;
 415: 
 416:       if (idTmdb == null) {
 417:         final searchUri = Uri.parse(
 418:             'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/search/$tipo?api_key=$tmdbApiKey&language=pt-BR&query=${Uri.encodeQueryComponent(obra.titulo)}');
 419:         final sResp =
 420:             await http.get(searchUri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 6));
 421:         if (sResp.statusCode == 200) {
 422:           final results =
 423:               (jsonDecode(utf8.decode(sResp.bodyBytes))['results'] as List?) ??
 424:                   [];
 425:           if (results.isNotEmpty) {
 426:             idTmdb = results.first['id'] as int?;
 427:           }
 428:         }
 429:       }
 430: 
 431:       if (idTmdb != null) {
 432:         final detUri = Uri.parse(
 433:             'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/$tipo/$idTmdb?api_key=$tmdbApiKey&language=pt-BR&append_to_response=credits');
 434:         final dResp =
 435:             await http.get(detUri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 6));
 436:         if (dResp.statusCode == 200) {
 437:           final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(dResp.bodyBytes));
 438:           final orig =
 439:               (data['original_title'] ?? data['original_name'] ?? '').toString();
 440:           final countries = (data['production_countries'] as List?) ?? [];
 441:           final paisNome = countries.isNotEmpty
 442:               ? (countries.first['name'] ?? '').toString()
 443:               : '';
 444:           final credits = data['credits'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
 445:           final cast = (credits['cast'] as List?) ?? [];
 446:           final crew = (credits['crew'] as List?) ?? [];
 447: 
 448:           final diretores = crew
 449:               .where((c) => (c['job'] ?? '') == 'Director')
 450:               .map((c) => (c['name'] ?? '').toString())
 451:               .where((n) => n.isNotEmpty)
 452:               .take(2)
 453:               .join(', ');
 454:           final criadores = ((data['created_by'] as List?) ?? [])
 455:               .map((c) => (c['name'] ?? '').toString())
 456:               .where((n) => n.isNotEmpty)
 457:               .take(2)
 458:               .join(', ');
 459:           final elencoStr = cast
 460:               .map((c) => (c['name'] ?? '').toString())
 461:               .where((n) => n.isNotEmpty)
 462:               .take(5)
 463:               .join(', ');
 464:           final sinopseTmdb = (data['overview'] ?? '').toString();
 465:           final vote = (data['vote_average'] is num)
 466:               ? (data['vote_average'] as num).toDouble()
 467:               : 0.0;
 468: 
 469:           final info = {
 470:             'tituloOriginal': orig,
 471:             'pais': paisNome,
 472:             'diretor': diretores.isNotEmpty ? diretores : criadores,
 473:             'elenco': elencoStr,
 474:             'sinopse': sinopseTmdb,
 475:             'nota': double.parse(vote.toStringAsFixed(1)),
 476:           };
 477:           _cacheCreditosTmdb[obra.chaveGrupo] = info;
 478:           return obra.copyWith(
 479:             tituloOriginal: orig,
 480:             pais: paisNome,
 481:             diretor: info['diretor'] as String,
 482:             elenco: elencoStr,
 483:             sinopse: sinopseTmdb,
 484:             notaImdb: info['nota'] as double,
 485:           );
 486:         }
 487:       }
 488:     } catch (_) {}
 489:     return obra;
 490:   }
 491: 
 492:   // =========================================================================
 493:   // BUSCA UNIFICADA (TÍTULOS + ELENCO + DIRETORES VIA TMDB)
 494:   // =========================================================================
 495:   static Future<List<ObraAgrupada>> buscarComElencoEDiretores(
 496:       String query, List<ObraAgrupada> catalogo) async {
 497:     final qNorm = normalizarTexto(query.trim());
 498:     if (qNorm.isEmpty) return [];
 499: 
 500:     final Map<String, ObraAgrupada> resultados = {};
 501:     for (final o in catalogo) {
 502:       final tit = normalizarTexto(o.titulo);
 503:       final orig = normalizarTexto(o.tituloOriginal);
 504:       final dir = normalizarTexto(o.diretor);
 505:       final el = normalizarTexto(o.elenco);
 506:       final gen = normalizarTexto(o.genero);
 507:       if (tit.contains(qNorm) ||
 508:           orig.contains(qNorm) ||
 509:           dir.contains(qNorm) ||
 510:           el.contains(qNorm) ||
 511:           gen.contains(qNorm)) {
 512:         resultados[o.chaveGrupo] = o;
 513:       }
 514:     }
 515: 
 516:     // Busca pessoa (ator/diretor) no TMDB para cruzar com o catálogo
 517:     if (qNorm.length >= 3) {
 518:       try {
 519:         final pUri = Uri.parse(
 520:             'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/search/person?api_key=$tmdbApiKey&language=pt-BR&query=${Uri.encodeQueryComponent(query.trim())}');
 521:         final resp = await http.get(pUri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 522:         if (resp.statusCode == 200) {
 523:           final pessoas =
 524:               (jsonDecode(utf8.decode(resp.bodyBytes))['results'] as List?) ??
 525:                   [];
 526:           final Set<int> idsRelacionados = {};
 527:           final Set<String> titulosRelacionados = {};
 528: 
 529:           for (final p in pessoas.take(2)) {
 530:             final known = (p['known_for'] as List?) ?? [];
 531:             for (final k in known) {
 532:               if (k['id'] is int) idsRelacionados.add(k['id']);
 533:               final t1 = normalizarTexto((k['title'] ?? '').toString());
 534:               final t2 = normalizarTexto((k['name'] ?? '').toString());
 535:               if (t1.isNotEmpty) titulosRelacionados.add(t1);
 536:               if (t2.isNotEmpty) titulosRelacionados.add(t2);
 537:             }
 538:             if (p['id'] is int) {
 539:               final credUri = Uri.parse(
 540:                   'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/person/${p['id']}/combined_credits?api_key=$tmdbApiKey&language=pt-BR');
 541:               final cResp =
 542:                   await http.get(credUri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 543:               if (cResp.statusCode == 200) {
 544:                 final cData = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(cResp.bodyBytes));
 545:                 for (final item in [
 546:                   ...((cData['cast'] as List?) ?? []),
 547:                   ...((cData['crew'] as List?) ?? []),
 548:                 ]) {
 549:                   if (item['id'] is int) idsRelacionados.add(item['id']);
 550:                   final t1 = normalizarTexto((item['title'] ?? '').toString());
 551:                   final t2 = normalizarTexto((item['name'] ?? '').toString());
 552:                   if (t1.isNotEmpty) titulosRelacionados.add(t1);
 553:                   if (t2.isNotEmpty) titulosRelacionados.add(t2);
 554:                 }
 555:               }
 556:             }
 557:           }
 558: 
 559:           for (final o in catalogo) {
 560:             if ((o.tmdbId != null && idsRelacionados.contains(o.tmdbId)) ||
 561:                 titulosRelacionados.contains(normalizarTexto(o.titulo))) {
 562:               resultados[o.chaveGrupo] = o;
 563:             }
 564:           }
 565:         }
 566:       } catch (_) {}
 567:     }
 568: 
 569:     return resultados.values.toList();
 570:   }
 571: 
 572:   // =========================================================================
 573:   // TOP 100 IMDb & TMDB POR CATEGORIA (INCLUI TÍTULOS FORA DO CATÁLOGO)
 574:   // =========================================================================
 575:   static Future<List<ObraAgrupada>> carregarTop100Ranking({
 576:     required String fonte, // 'imdb' ou 'tmdb'
 577:     required String categoria, // 'Filmes', 'Séries', 'Kids', 'Anime'
 578:     required List<ObraAgrupada> catalogoLocal,
 579:   }) async {
 580:     final isTv = categoria == 'Séries' || categoria == 'Anime';
 581:     final endpoint = isTv ? 'tv' : 'movie';
 582:     final sort = fonte == 'imdb'
 583:         ? 'vote_average.desc&vote_count.gte=${isTv ? 400 : 1200}'
 584:         : 'popularity.desc';
 585: 
 586:     String extraGenre = '';
 587:     if (categoria == 'Kids') extraGenre = '&with_genres=10751,16';
 588:     if (categoria == 'Anime') {
 589:       extraGenre = '&with_genres=16&with_original_language=ja';
 590:     }
 591: 
 592:     final Map<String, ObraAgrupada> mapaLocalPorTitulo = {};
 593:     final Map<int, ObraAgrupada> mapaLocalPorId = {};
 594:     for (final o in catalogoLocal) {
 595:       mapaLocalPorTitulo[normalizarTexto(o.titulo)] = o;
 596:       if (o.tmdbId != null) mapaLocalPorId[o.tmdbId!] = o;
 597:     }
 598: 
 599:     final List<ObraAgrupada> ranking = [];
 600:     try {
 601:       // 5 páginas x 20 itens = Top 100
 602:       final futures = List.generate(5, (idx) {
 603:         final page = idx + 1;
 604:         final uri = Uri.parse(
 605:             'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/discover/$endpoint?api_key=$tmdbApiKey&language=pt-BR&sort_by=$sort$extraGenre&page=$page');
 606:         return http.get(uri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 8));
 607:       });
 608: 
 609:       final responses = await Future.wait(futures);
 610:       for (final resp in responses) {
 611:         if (resp.statusCode != 200) continue;
 612:         final results =
 613:             (jsonDecode(utf8.decode(resp.bodyBytes))['results'] as List?) ?? [];
 614:         for (final item in results) {
 615:           if (ranking.length >= 100) break;
 616:           final int? id = item['id'] is int ? item['id'] : null;
 617:           final titulo =
 618:               (item['title'] ?? item['name'] ?? 'Sem Título').toString();
 619:           final normTit = normalizarTexto(titulo);
 620: 
 621:           // Se já existir no seu catálogo do Supabase, vincula com os streams reais
 622:           if (id != null && mapaLocalPorId.containsKey(id)) {
 623:             ranking.add(mapaLocalPorId[id]!);
 624:             continue;
 625:           }
 626:           if (mapaLocalPorTitulo.containsKey(normTit)) {
 627:             ranking.add(mapaLocalPorTitulo[normTit]!);
 628:             continue;
 629:           }
 630: 
 631:           final poster = (item['poster_path'] ?? '').toString();
 632:           final backdrop = (item['backdrop_path'] ?? '').toString();
 633:           final dataLanc =
 634:               (item['release_date'] ?? item['first_air_date'] ?? '').toString();
 635:           final ano = dataLanc.length >= 4 ? dataLanc.substring(0, 4) : '';
 636:           final nota = (item['vote_average'] is num)
 637:               ? double.parse((item['vote_average'] as num).toStringAsFixed(1))
 638:               : 0.0;
 639: 
 640:           ranking.add(ObraAgrupada(
 641:             chaveGrupo: 'rank_${fonte}_${categoria}_${id ?? ranking.length}',
 642:             tmdbId: id,
 643:             titulo: titulo,
 644:             tituloOriginal:
 645:                 (item['original_title'] ?? item['original_name'] ?? '')
 646:                     .toString(),
 647:             categoria: categoria,
 648:             genero: categoria,
 649:             sinopse: (item['overview'] ?? '').toString(),
 650:             capaUrl: poster.isNotEmpty
 651:                 ? 'https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w500$poster'
 652:                 : '',
 653:             bannerUrl: backdrop.isNotEmpty
 654:                 ? 'https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w1280$backdrop'
 655:                 : '',
 656:             ano: ano,
 657:             emDestaque: false,
 658:             ordemDestaque: ranking.length + 1,
 659:             notaImdb: nota,
 660:             fontes: const [], // Ainda não adicionado no catálogo (exibe badge "Em breve")
 661:           ));
 662:         }
 663:       }
 664:     } catch (_) {}
 665: 
 666:     return ranking;
 667:   }
 668: 
 669: }

  26: class TorrServerService {

  28:   static const String baseUrl = 'http://127.0.0.1:8090';
  29:   static Process? _process;
  30:   static bool _configurado = false;

  33:   static Future<bool> _portaAberta() async {
  34:     try {
  35:       final s = await Socket.connect('127.0.0.1', 8090, timeout: const Duration(milliseconds: 350));
  36:       s.destroy();
  37:       return true;
  38:     } catch (_) {
  39:       return false;
  40:     }
  41:   }

  43:   static Future<void> _aplicarConfiguracoes() async {
  44:     if (_configurado) return;
  45:     try {
  46:       await _postJson('/settings', {
  47:         'action': 'set',
  48:         'sets': {
  49:           'CacheSize': 67108864,
  50:           'ReaderReadAHead': 95,
  51:           'PreloadCache': 2,
  52:           'UseDisk': false,
  53:           'ForceEncrypt': false,
  54:           'RetrackersMode': 1,
  55:           'TorrentDisconnectTimeout': 30,
  56:           'EnableIPv6': true,
  57:           'DisableTCP': false,
  58:           'DisableUTP': false,
  59:           'DisableUPNP': false,
  60:           'DisableDHT': false,
  61:           'DisablePEX': false,
  62:           'DisableUpload': false,
  63:           'ConnectionsLimit': 150,
  64:           'PeersListenPort': 0,
  65:         },
  66:       });
  67:       _configurado = true;
  68:     } catch (_) {}
  69:   }
  70: 
  71:   static Future<bool> garantirAtivo() async {
  72:     if (await _portaAberta()) {
  73:       await _aplicarConfiguracoes();
  74:       return true;
  75:     }
  76:     try {
  77:       final paths = await _channel.invokeMapMethod('getPaths');
  78:       final nativeLibDir = (paths?['nativeLibDir'] ?? '').toString();
  79:       final filesDir = (paths?['filesDir'] ?? '').toString();
  80:       final binPath = '$nativeLibDir/libtorrserver.so';
  81:       if (nativeLibDir.isEmpty || !await File(binPath).exists()) {
  82:         ultimoErro = 'Motor de streaming local não encontrado.';
  83:         return false;
  84:       }
  85:       final dataDir = Directory('$filesDir/torrserver');
  86:       if (!await dataDir.exists()) await dataDir.create(recursive: true);
  87: 
  88:       final tentativas = [
  89:         ['-p', '8090', '-d', dataDir.path],
  90:         ['--port', '8090', '--path', dataDir.path],
  91:         ['']..clear(),
  92:       ];
  93: 
  94:       for (final args in tentativas) {
  95:         _process?.kill();
  96:         _process = await Process.start(
  97:           binPath,
  98:           args,
  99:           workingDirectory: dataDir.path,
 100:           environment: {
 101:             'HOME': filesDir,
 102:             'TMPDIR': dataDir.path,
 103:             'RES_OPTIONS': 'ndots:0',
 104:             'GODEBUG': 'netdns=go',
 105:           },
 106:         );
 107:         for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
 108:           await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 200));
 109:           if (await _portaAberta()) {
 110:             ultimoErro = '';
 111:             await _aplicarConfiguracoes();
 112:             return true;
 113:           }
 114:         }
 115:       }
 116:       ultimoErro = 'Tempo esgotado ao iniciar o motor local.';
 117:       return false;
 118:     } catch (e) {
 119:       ultimoErro = 'Erro ao iniciar motor local: $e';
 120:       return false;
 121:     }
 122:   }
 123: 
 124:   static Future _postJson(String endpoint, Map body) async {
 125:     final c = HttpClient()..connectionTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 4);
 126:     try {
 127:       final req = await c.postUrl(Uri.parse('$baseUrl$endpoint'));
 128:       req.headers.contentType = ContentType.json;
 129:       req.write(jsonEncode(body));
 130:       final res = await req.close().timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 131:       final txt = await utf8.decoder.bind(res).join();
 132:       if (txt.trim().startsWith('{')) return jsonDecode(txt);
 133:     } catch (_) {} finally {
 134:       c.close(force: true);
 135:     }
 136:     return null;
 137:   }
 138: 
 139:   static Future<List<int>?> _baixarTorrentCache(String hashHex) async {
 140:     final hUpper = hashHex.toUpperCase();
 141:     final urls = [
 142:       'https://itorrents.org/torrent/$hUpper.torrent',
 143:       'https://torrage.info/torrent.php?h=$hUpper',
 144:       'https://btcache.me/torrent/$hUpper',
 145:     ];
 146:     for (final u in urls) {
 147:       final c = HttpClient()..connectionTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 4);
 148:       try {
 149:         final req = await c.getUrl(Uri.parse(u));
 150:         req.headers.set('User-Agent', 'Mozilla/5.0');
 151:         final res = await req.close().timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 152:         if (res.statusCode == 200) {
 153:           final bytes = await res.fold<List<int>>([], (prev, el) => prev..addAll(el));
 154:           if (bytes.length > 100 && bytes.first == 100) return bytes;
 155:         } else {
 156:           await res.drain();
 157:         }
 158:       } catch (_) {} finally {
 159:         c.close(force: true);
 160:       }
 161:     }
 162:     return null;
 163:   }
 164: 
 165:   static Future<bool> _uploadTorrentParaServidor(List<int> bytes, String hashHex) async {
 166:     final c = HttpClient()..connectionTimeout = const Duration(seconds: 4);
 167:     try {
 168:       const boundary = '----TorrServerBoundary7MA4YWxkTrZu0gW';
 169:       final req = await c.postUrl(Uri.parse('$baseUrl/torrent/upload'));
 170:       req.headers.set('Content-Type', 'multipart/form-data; boundary=$boundary');
 171:       final header = '--$boundary\r\n'
 172:           'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="file"; filename="$hashHex.torrent"\r\n'
 173:           'Content-Type: application/x-bittorrent\r\n\r\n';
 174:       const footer = '\r\n--$boundary--\r\n';
 175:       req.add(utf8.encode(header));
 176:       req.add(bytes);
 177:       req.add(utf8.encode(footer));
 178:       final res = await req.close().timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));
 179:       await res.drain();
 180:       return res.statusCode >= 200 && res.statusCode < 300;
 181:     } catch (_) {
 182:       return false;
 183:     } finally {
 184:       c.close(force: true);
 185:     }
 186:   }
 187: 
 188:   static String _base32ParaHex(String b32) {
 189:     const alpha = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567';
 190:     final clean = b32.toUpperCase().replaceAll('=', '');
 191:     int bits = 0, val = 0;
 192:     final bytes = [];
 193:     for (int i = 0; i < clean.length; i++) {
 194:       final idx = alpha.indexOf(clean[i]);
 195:       if (idx < 0) return b32.toLowerCase();
 196:       val = (val << 5) | idx;
 197:       bits += 5;
 198:       if (bits >= 8) {
 199:         bytes.add((val >> (bits - 8)) & 0xFF);
 200:         bits -= 8;
 201:       }
 202:     }
 203:     return bytes.map((b) => (b as int).toRadixString(16).padLeft(2, '0')).join();
 204:   }
 205: 
 206:   static String _extrairHash(String magnet) {
 207:     final m = RegExp(r'btih:([a-zA-Z0-9]{32,40})', caseSensitive: false).firstMatch(magnet);
 208:     if (m == null) return '';
 209:     final raw = m.group(1)!;
 210:     return raw.length == 32 ? _base32ParaHex(raw) : raw.toLowerCase();
 211:   }
 212: 
 213:   static String _enriquecerMagnet(String magnet) {
 214:     var clean = magnet.trim().replaceFirstMapped(
 215:       RegExp(r'(btih:)([a-zA-Z2-7]{32})(?=&|$)', caseSensitive: false),
 216:       (m) => '${m.group(1)}${_base32ParaHex(m.group(2)!)}',
 217:     );
 218:     const trackers = [
 219:       'udp://tracker.opentrackr.org:1337/announce',
 220:       'http://tracker.opentrackr.org:1337/announce',
 221:       'udp://open.stealth.si:80/announce',
 222:       'udp://tracker.torrent.eu.org:451/announce',
 223:       'udp://open.demonii.com:1337/announce',
 224:       'udp://tracker.openbittorrent.com:6969/announce',
 225:       'http://tracker.openbittorrent.com:80/announce',
 226:       'udp://exodus.desync.com:6969/announce',
 227:       'udp://tracker.moeking.me:6969/announce',
 228:       'udp://explodie.org:6969/announce',
 229:       'udp://tracker.tiny-vps.com:6969/announce',
 230:       'udp://tracker.dler.org:6969/announce',
 231:       'https://tracker.tamersunion.org:443/announce',
 232:       'https://tracker.loligirl.cn:443/announce',
 233:       'https://tracker.gbitt.info:443/announce',
 234:       'http://93.158.213.92:1337/announce',
 235:       'udp://93.158.213.92:1337/announce',
 236:       'udp://tracker1.bt.moack.co.kr:80/announce',
 237:       'udp://tracker.theoks.net:6969/announce',
 238:       'udp://tracker.bittor.pw:1337/announce',
 239:       'udp://movies.zsw.ca:6969/announce',
 240:       'udp://tracker.cyberia.is:6969/announce',
 241:       'udp://retracker01-msk-virt.corbina.net:80/announce',
 242:       'udp://p4p.arenabg.com:1337/announce',
 243:       'udp://tracker.leechers-paradise.org:6969/announce',
 244:       'udp://tracker.coppersurfer.tk:6969/announce',
 245:       'udp://9.rarbg.com:2810/announce',
 246:       'udp://tracker.publictracker.xyz:6969/announce',
 247:     ];
 248:     final sb = StringBuffer(clean);
 249:     final lower = clean.toLowerCase();
 250:     for (final tr in trackers) {
 251:       final enc = Uri.encodeComponent(tr).toLowerCase();
 252:       if (!lower.contains(enc) && !lower.contains(tr.toLowerCase())) {
 253:         sb.write('&tr=${Uri.encodeComponent(tr)}');
 254:       }
 255:     }
 256:     return sb.toString();
 257:   }
 258: 
 259:   static Future<TorrentStreamInfo?> prepararMagnet(
 260:     String magnetUrl, {
 261:     void Function(String)? onStatus,
 262:     void Function(double)? onProgress,
 263:     bool Function()? cancelado,
 264:   }) async {
 265:     ultimoErro = '';
 266:     final cleanMagnet = _enriquecerMagnet(magnetUrl);
 267:     final hash = _extrairHash(cleanMagnet);
 268:     onProgress?.call(0.12);
 269:     onStatus?.call('Iniciando motor de vídeo...');
 270:     if (!await garantirAtivo()) return null;
 271:     if (cancelado != null && cancelado()) return null;
 272: 
 273:     onProgress?.call(0.25);
 274:     onStatus?.call('Verificando fontes e trackers...');
 275:     bool subiuTorrent = false;
 276:     if (hash.length == 40) {
 277:       final torrentBytes = await _baixarTorrentCache(hash);
 278:       if (torrentBytes != null) {
 279:         subiuTorrent = await _uploadTorrentParaServidor(torrentBytes, hash);
 280:       }
 281:     }
 282: 
 283:     await _postJson('/torrents', {
 284:       'action': 'add',
 285:       'link': cleanMagnet,
 286:       'title': 'Stream',
 287:       'save_to_db': false,
 288:     });
 289: 
 290:     int fileId = 1;
 291:     String fileName = 'video.mkv';
 292:     int fileSize = 0;
 293:     int activePeers = 0;
 294:     int totalSeeds = 0;
 295:     double speedBytes = 0;
 296:     bool encontrouArquivo = false;
 297: 
 298:     for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
 299:       if (cancelado != null && cancelado()) return null;
 300:       await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 500));
 301:       onProgress?.call((0.30 + (i * 0.018)).clamp(0.30, 0.88));
 302:       final info = await _postJson('/torrents', {'action': 'get', 'hash': hash});
 303:       if (info is Map) {
 304:         activePeers = (info['active_peers'] is num) ? (info['active_peers'] as num).toInt() : 0;
 305:         totalSeeds = (info['connected_seeders'] is num)
 306:             ? (info['connected_seeders'] as num).toInt()
 307:             : ((info['total_peers'] is num) ? (info['total_peers'] as num).toInt() : 0);
 308:         speedBytes = (info['download_speed'] is num) ? (info['download_speed'] as num).toDouble() : 0.0;
 309: 
 310:         final files = info['file_stats'];
 311:         if (files is List && files.isNotEmpty) {
 312:           Map? maior;
 313:           for (final f in files) {
 314:             if (f is Map) {
 315:               final p = (f['path'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();
 316:               final ehVid = p.endsWith('.mkv') || p.endsWith('.mp4') || p.endsWith('.avi') || p.endsWith('.ts');
 317:               if (ehVid && (maior == null || (f['length'] ?? 0) > (maior['length'] ?? 0))) {
 318:                 maior = f;
 319:               }
 320:             }
 321:           }
 322:           maior ??= files.first as Map;
 323:           fileId = (maior['id'] is int) ? maior['id'] as int : 1;
 324:           fileName = (maior['path'] ?? 'video.mkv').toString().split('/').last;
 325:           fileSize = (maior['length'] is int) ? maior['length'] as int : 0;
 326:           encontrouArquivo = true;
 327:           if (activePeers > 0 || totalSeeds > 0 || i >= 8) break;
 328:         }
 329:       }
 330:       if (i >= 14 && !encontrouArquivo && activePeers == 0 && totalSeeds == 0) break;
 331:       onStatus?.call('Buscando peers ativos (peers: $activePeers)...');
 332:     }
 333: 
 334:     if (!encontrouArquivo || (activePeers == 0 && totalSeeds == 0 && !subiuTorrent)) {
 335:       ultimoErro = 'Fonte sem peers ativos no momento.';
 336:       return null;
 337:     }
 338: 
 339:     onProgress?.call(0.94);
 340:     onStatus?.call('Iniciando reprodução...');
 341:     return TorrentStreamInfo(
 342:       playUrl: '$baseUrl/play/$hash/$fileId?play',
 343:       fileName: fileName,
 344:       fileSizeBytes: fileSize,
 345:       activePeers: activePeers,
 346:       totalSeeds: totalSeeds,
 347:       downloadSpeedBytes: speedBytes,
 348:     );
 349:   }
 350: }

 422: 
 423: class CinemaFocusBox extends StatefulWidget {
 424:   final Widget child;
 425:   final VoidCallback? onTap;
 426:   final double borderRadius;
 427:   final double scaleOnFocus;
 428:   final bool autofocus;
 429:   final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;
 430:   final ValueChanged<bool>? onFocusChanged;
 431: 
 432:   const CinemaFocusBox({
 433:     super.key,
 434:     required this.child,
 435:     this.onTap,
 436:     this.borderRadius = 12,
 437:     this.scaleOnFocus = 1.045,
 438:     this.autofocus = false,
 439:     this.padding = EdgeInsets.zero,
 440:     this.onFocusChanged,
 441:   });
 442: 
 443:   @override
 444:   State<CinemaFocusBox> createState() => _CinemaFocusBoxState();
 445: }
 446: 
 447: class _CinemaFocusBoxState extends State<CinemaFocusBox> {
 448:   bool _focused = false;
 449:   bool _hovered = false;
 450:   DateTime _ultimoAcionamento = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);
 451: 
 452:   bool get _ativo => _focused || _hovered;
 453: 
 454:   void _acionar() {
 455:     if (widget.onTap == null) return;
 456:     final agora = DateTime.now();
 457:     if (agora.difference(_ultimoAcionamento).inMilliseconds < 280) return;
 458:     _ultimoAcionamento = agora;
 459:     widget.onTap!();
 460:   }
 461: 
 462:   bool _isConfirmKey(LogicalKeyboardKey key) {
 463:     return key == LogicalKeyboardKey.select ||
 464:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.enter ||
 465:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.numpadEnter ||
 466:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.accept ||
 467:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.gameButtonA ||
 468:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.space ||
 469:         key == LogicalKeyboardKey.mediaPlayPause;
 470:   }
 471: 
 472:   @override
 473:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 474:     return MouseRegion(
 475:       cursor: widget.onTap != null ? SystemMouseCursors.click : MouseCursor.defer,
 476:       onEnter: (_) => setState(() => _hovered = true),
 477:       onExit: (_) => setState(() => _hovered = false),
 478:       child: Actions(
 479:         actions: <Type, Action<Intent>>{
 480:           ActivateIntent: CallbackAction<ActivateIntent>(
 481:             onInvoke: (_) {
 482:               _acionar();
 483:               return null;
 484:             },
 485:           ),
 486:           ButtonActivateIntent: CallbackAction<ButtonActivateIntent>(
 487:             onInvoke: (_) {
 488:               _acionar();
 489:               return null;
 490:             },
 491:           ),
 492:         },
 493:         child: Focus(
 494:           autofocus: widget.autofocus,
 495:           onFocusChange: (f) {
 496:             setState(() => _focused = f);
 497:             widget.onFocusChanged?.call(f);
 498:           },
 499:           onKeyEvent: (node, event) {
 500:             if (_isConfirmKey(event.logicalKey)) {
 501:               if (event is KeyDownEvent) {
 502:                 _acionar();
 503:               }
 504:               return KeyEventResult.handled;
 505:             }
 506:             return KeyEventResult.ignored;
 507:           },
 508:           child: GestureDetector(
 509:             onTap: _acionar,
 510:             child: AnimatedScale(
 511:               scale: _ativo ? widget.scaleOnFocus : 1.0,
 512:               duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 140),
 513:               curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
 514:               child: AnimatedContainer(
 515:                 duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 140),
 516:                 padding: widget.padding,
 517:                 decoration: BoxDecoration(
 518:                   borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(widget.borderRadius),
 519:                   border: Border.all(
 520:                     color: _ativo ? const Color(0xFFF5B82E) : Colors.transparent,
 521:                     width: _ativo ? 3.0 : 1.0,
 522:                   ),
 523:                   boxShadow: _ativo
 524:                       ? [
 525:                           BoxShadow(
 526:                             color: const Color(0xFFF5B82E).withOpacity(0.50),
 527:                             blurRadius: 16,
 528:                             spreadRadius: 1.5,
 529:                           ),
 530:                         ]
 531:                       : const [],
 532:                 ),
 533:                 child: widget.child,
 534:               ),
 535:             ),
 536:           ),
 537:         ),
 538:       ),
 539:     );
 540:   }
 541: }

ARQUIVO: make_icon.py (42 linhas)
===========================================================================
   1: import os, struct, zlib, math
   2: 
   3: def make_png(size, path):
   4:     w = h = size
   5:     cx = cy = w / 2.0
   6:     rows = []
   7:     for y in range(h):
   8:         row = bytearray([0])
   9:         for x in range(w):
  10:             # Fundo gradiente escuro
  11:             dx, dy = (x - cx) / w, (y - cy) / h
  12:             dist = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy)
  13:             r = max(11, int(36 - dist * 35))
  14:             g = max(12, int(14 - dist * 10))
  15:             b = max(18, int(24 - dist * 12))
  16: 
  17:             # Anel dourado/vermelho sutil
  18:             if 0.38 < dist < 0.41:
  19:                 r, g, b = 229, 9, 20
  20: 
  21:             # Triangulo Play central branco/vermelho
  22:             nx, ny = (x - cx) / (w * 0.26), (y - cy) / (h * 0.26)
  23:             if -0.55 <= nx <= 0.75:
  24:                 lim = 0.72 * (1.0 - (nx + 0.55) / 1.30)
  25:                 if abs(ny) <= lim:
  26:                     r, g, b = 255, 255, 255
  27:                     if nx > 0.15:
  28:                         r, g, b = 255, 215, 0
  29: 
  30:             row.extend((r, g, b, 255))
  31:         rows.append(bytes(row))
  32:     raw = b"".join(rows)
  33:     def chunk(tag, data):
  34:         return struct.pack("!I", len(data)) + tag + data + struct.pack("!I", zlib.crc32(tag + data) & 0xffffffff)
  35:     ihdr = struct.pack("!IIBBBBB", w, h, 8, 6, 0, 0, 0)
  36:     os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
  37:     with open(path, "wb") as f:
  38:         f.write(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n" + chunk(b"IHDR", ihdr) + chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(raw, 9)) + chunk(b"IEND", b""))
  39: 
  40: for folder, sz in [("mipmap-mdpi", 48), ("mipmap-hdpi", 72), ("mipmap-xhdpi", 96), ("mipmap-xxhdpi", 144), ("mipmap-xxxhdpi", 192)]:
  41:     make_png(sz, f"android/app/src/main/res/{folder}/ic_launcher.png")
  42:     make_png(sz, f"android/app/src/main/res/{folder}/ic_launcher_round.png")
